- **Smart Join Practice / Run Now button** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback, `findActiveLiveSession` + `isSessionLive` state added to `HomeScreen.jsx`, `PracticeDetail.jsx`, and Builder's `BuilderScreen` in `App.jsx`): any Run Now/Start Practice button now checks whether a live session already exists for that practice and, if so, relabels itself "Join Practice" and routes into the existing session (`/run/:practiceId`) instead of racing to create a new one — this is the direct fix for the reported bug (an assistant's "Run Practice" tap force-starting the drill timer out from under the head coach mid-setup). Home's hero button row was also simplified from a 3-way Practice Setup/Review Plan/Start Practice conditional split down to a fixed `[Review Plan][smart primary button]` pair, since Practice Setup is no longer a separate destination from the live session itself (see the Live Sessions section above).
- **Home hero's "nothing on the schedule" empty state, made defensive** (thirty-second session continued): direct feedback described the hero showing an empty-schedule message while the Schedule tab visibly had practices on it — code review found no reproducible date-window bug behind this specific framing (the user wasn't sure of the exact repro either), so rather than guess at or remove the unrelated per-team `show_on_home` toggle, a `nextCancelledPractice` fallback was added: when there's no plannable next practice but the soonest upcoming one is cancelled, the hero now shows a "View Practice" card for it instead of the flat "nothing scheduled" empty state, which was the one confirmed case where the old copy could read as misleading.
- **`GettingStartedCard` no longer flashes on every Home load for a coach who already completed it** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback): `hasCompleted`'s initial state changed from `false` to `null` (loading/unknown, not "not done yet"), and `checklistDone` now treats `null` as "don't render the incomplete-checklist UI yet" — previously the card's own fetch hadn't resolved on first paint, so every load briefly rendered the full checklist (since `false` looked identical to "genuinely incomplete") before flipping to hidden a moment later once the real completed-status came back.
- **Schedule tab**: Agenda (day-grouped, team-color-coded dots) and Month (calendar grid with per-day dots) views, both using the same hollow-when-unplanned/filled-when-planned dot rule. **Agenda's own "Show/Hide Completed / History" section and its `showPast` state are removed** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback — history now lives solely in Goals & Insights' rebuilt History view, see that section); Month view keeps its existing past-practice access via `DaySheet`/`HistoryViewer` unchanged, since the request was scoped specifically to the Agenda tab.
- **Planned absences**: capture from Practice Detail, Player Profile, or a Home quick action. Feeds Builder's default group assignment and the live session's attendance defaults. **Enforced inside station-block editing too, not just at add-time** (thirty-second session, direct feedback): `StationConfig` (`ActivityConfigs.jsx`) now takes `absentPlayerIds` and disables that player's chip in every station's player picker (grayed out, strikethrough name, "Out" badge, `title` tooltip, `cursor:not-allowed`) rather than only relying on the assignment defaults computed when the activity was first added — a player marked out *after* a station block already had assignments now also gets auto-removed from any station they were sitting in, via an effect keyed on the absent-id set. `genRandom`/`groupByPosition`/`groupByHand` all draw from an `assignablePlayers` list with the absent players already filtered out, so neither random nor attribute-based grouping can assign an out player either.
- Cancel (this-occurrence or this-and-future for a series) / restore; a "missed" state for practices whose time passed with no run.
- All practice times display in the **team's** timezone, never the viewing device's; "today" is always computed via `localDateStr()` (local calendar day), never `.toISOString()`.
- **Planning-depth indicator**: any practice with a `scheduled_duration_minutes` shows "35/60 min" whether planned or not. Three states, purely derived (`planningState()`/`sumMins()`), never stored: red under 90% planned, green at or above it, and — added twenty-seventh session, direct feedback — amber/orange ("exceeds") when the planned drills actually add up to *more* than the scheduled duration, distinct from the under-planned case since running long is a different problem worth its own color. The green state keeps a `✓` glyph; red/amber deliberately have **no** icon (see Session Log for why not `⚠`). Builder's own duration pill (`.pill.over`/now also `.pill.exceeds`) got the same third state.
- **Getting-started checklist** (reworked seventeenth session): was a "?"-icon popup with a green-dot nudge and static, non-clickable rows — direct feedback was that a brand-new coach's first Home screen wasn't intuitive about how to proceed. Now a persistent `GettingStartedCard` on Home itself (gone once every step is done), same 6 steps (team → players → library → schedule → first plan → run live), but each row (except "Run it live," which has no single screen of its own to jump to) is real navigation to the right screen. The green dot on "?" is gone since the card itself is now the visible nudge; "Getting Started" was also removed from the "?" menu (now just FAQs + Send Feedback) since the checklist isn't a popup anymore. **Hide/show added forty-second session**: a "Hide" control on the card itself (`setGettingStartedHidden`, per-coach `localStorage`, read synchronously on mount so it never flashes before disappearing) plus a "Getting Started" row in Settings' top-level list that clears the hidden flag and returns to Home -- a no-op if every step is already done, since Home's own `!checklistDone` gate still applies regardless. **"Plan your first practice" step gated on real ability, forty-fourth session** (direct feedback: "an assistant... shouldn't see a button for planning" if it hasn't been delegated to them): the step's CTA now only navigates when a new `canPlanAny` check passes (head-coach or explicitly `canBuildPractices`-delegated on at least one team/org), otherwise it renders inert — an assistant with no planning delegation anywhere no longer gets a button that goes nowhere useful.
- **New `/faq` page** (seventeenth session, `LegalPages.jsx`, same top-level-sibling-route treatment as Terms/Privacy): natural-voice accordion FAQ, includes "Can I delete my account?" (deactivation vs. contact-us-to-actually-delete) and "How do I set up an organization with multiple teams?" (links to the consultation request above).
- **Missing-location dialogs** (seventeenth session): a coach with zero locations had no inline way to add one from Builder (empty `<select>`, no placeholder) or from the quick-schedule modal (which already had a working "None" option, just no way to add a real one). Both now offer a shared `AddLocationDialog` (`NewLibraryScreen.jsx`, exported) — name + optional sublocations in one step, auto-selects the new location and lets the coach continue what they were doing. Ownership (personal vs. org) is decided by the specific team being scheduled/built for (`team.organizationId`), not the global Coach/Org mode toggle, so it works correctly even through Builder's own known-not-mode-scoped team picker.
- **SeriesWizard consolidated** (seventeenth session): was 5 sequential screens (team → days/time → date range → location → preview); the first 4 (everything that's just picking fields) are now one "details" screen, with only the review/per-date-deselect step kept separate. Team-picker defaulting to the team you opened it from (via `presetTeamId`) already worked correctly before this change — confirmed, not touched.
- **Schedule empty state**: the global Schedule tab now prompts "No teams yet" with a "+ Create a Team" button when `data.teams.length===0`, instead of an empty calendar with no obvious next step.
- **Practice plan PDF export** (2026-07-29, `PracticePlanPrint.jsx`, triggered from a "Print / Export PDF" button on Practice Detail): renders a standalone, print-optimized document — not a screenshot of the app UI — and hands off to the browser's native Print/Save-as-PDF (no client-side PDF library, no server round trip). `#rop-print-root` + a new `@media print` block in App.jsx's `CSS` constant isolate that one subtree regardless of what else is mounted. Every drill shows duration **and** a computed clock start/end range (anchored to the practice's own start time, walking cumulatively through `actMins()` — same formula already used on-screen, so the printed total never diverges from what's displayed in-app), plus description, coaching focus, skill tags, equipment, player-gear notes, grouping, and per-player focus notes (resolved the same category-matching way the live view already does it). Station blocks get the same treatment per station. Intentionally reuses already-loaded client `data`/`team` state rather than a new RPC — no new data-access surface, just a different render of what Practice Detail already has.
- **Multi-Coach Builder (forty-eighth session, 2026-08-16, reworked same day per direct feedback)**: a head coach can delegate individual stations of a station block to specific rostered coaches, who then edit only their own station's drill/notes/equipment independently, safely whether that happens days apart or at the same moment as another coach editing a different station. **Two genuinely separate fields, not one overloaded picker** — an early version reused the pre-existing `stations.team_staff_id` ("who leads this station live") to also mean "who's delegated to design it," which direct feedback correctly flagged as confusing (no way to tell which of the two you were setting). `stations.delegated_to` (new column) is the real one: who's asked to plan the station's content, settable only by the head coach via a new **"Plan This Station"** control in Builder (`ActivityConfigs.jsx` `StationConfig`, placed next to Choose from Library, not folded into the Coach field), offering only `can_build_practices` ("Share Practice Planning") coaches, showing a live **Planned / Needs Planning** status per delegated station (visible even collapsed, and rolled up per block as "N of M delegated stations planned"). `stations.team_staff_id` reverted to exactly what it was before this feature touched it — unrestricted live-leader picker, zero editing implication, untouched by any of this.
  - **The concurrency fix**: `update_station_content` (`security definer`) — one atomic per-station `UPDATE`, never a `savePracticeTree` full-tree pass — callable only by whoever `delegated_to` currently points at (re-checked fresh against `can_build_practices` on every call, not trusted from the client) or whoever `can_manage_team`. Re-derives the station's real current delegate/team from the DB every call, so a coach reassigned or an activity archived out from under an in-flight caller fails with a distinct `STATION_NOT_FOUND`/`NOT_AUTHORIZED`. Skeleton-level changes (add/remove/reorder a station, block timing, station count) stay head-coach-only through the existing full-tree save path, unchanged. `stations.station_updated_at`/`station_updated_by` surface "last saved N minutes ago" per station.
  - **`BuilderRoute` (`App.jsx`) branches per practice**: a non-manager coach delegated at least one station anywhere in that practice's activities (`delegated_to`, not `team_staff_id`) lands on `MyStationBuilderScreen` (`components/MyStationBuilder.jsx`) instead of the normal skeleton editor — their own station's edit form (reusing `AutoTextarea`/`equipmentPickerAssets`/`EquipmentPickerPill` from `ActivityConfigs.jsx`, not `StationConfig` itself, whose block-level/player-assignment state is too tightly interleaved to safely scope down) plus a read-only summary card (drill name, block duration, equipment, live leader, last-edited-by) for every other station in the same block, no skeleton controls anywhere. A full delegate with no station specifically delegated to them still gets the normal full `BuilderScreen`, unchanged. Polls `refreshPlanning()` every ~25s for sibling summary cards, matching Home's own staleness-backstop cadence rather than realtime. `PracticeDetail`'s Edit button also shows ("Build My Station") for a coach delegated a station but with no manage rights, so there's a repeat way back in beyond the one-time notice.
  - **Edge cases (handoff §7)**: `MyStationEditor`'s local draft is seeded once and never clobbered by a later poll; if the station is later un-delegated or deleted/archived, the editor shows an explicit banner (distinguishing the two) with the draft still visible and a "Copy My Notes" clipboard fallback, never a silent failed write.
  - **Presence**: a new per-station Realtime Presence channel (`subscribeToStationPresence`, keyed by `station_presence_<id>`) reusing the existing `subscribeToPracticePresence` mechanism's pattern without touching its three existing call sites. The delegate's own editor tracks into it while mounted; the head coach's skeleton view subscribes read-only per delegated station and shows a small "● editing now" indicator.
  - **Notification**: `station_assignment_notices` (mirrors `team_join_notices`' shape/RLS/ack pattern), populated by a DB trigger on `stations` (`delegated_to` insert/update, requiring `can_build_practices`) rather than a single RPC, since `delegated_to` can be written from more than one place. Surfaced as a Home card ("You've been asked to plan Station X for Y. Build My Station / Dismiss").
  - **Entitlements**: the old one-per-team unique index on `can_build_practices` is gone — Pro+ allows unlimited delegates per team (`entitlements.js` `PLAN_LIMITS.pro_plus.delegatedPlanningPerTeam` `1`→`null`, still fully inert until `BILLING_ENABLED`), since station delegation eligibility reuses `can_build_practices` as its gate.
  - **Two real bugs found live verifying this, same session**: the "stations planned" completion indicator's first version checked for a non-empty station name, but `addStation` seeds every new station's `name` with a non-empty "Station N" placeholder that `saveActivityTree` persists as the real DB value for an untouched station (there's only one `name` column, so the client-side `activityName`/`name` split doesn't survive a save) — fixed with `stationIsPlanned()` (`constants.js`, shared by both the per-station and block-level indicators), a combined heuristic (`libraryId` set, `stationUpdatedAt` set, any real content, or a name that isn't just the placeholder). Separately, `update_station_content`'s first version only checked "is the caller currently this station's assigned coach," not `can_build_practices` — meaning reassigning a station's *live* leader (the unrestricted picker) would have silently also granted real editing rights, until the field split made this moot (the live picker no longer writes the field the RPC checks at all).
  - Verified live end-to-end with the persistent QA accounts (`ropqa-head`/`ropqa-asst`, "QA Persistent Wolves") at every stage: direct RPC/RLS scripts confirmed simultaneous writes to different stations by different coaches land independently with correct attribution, a coach set only as live leader (not `delegated_to`) is correctly refused, `delegated_to` alone (independent of who's set as live leader) is sufficient and necessary to write, and both the un-delegation and archival edge cases fail with the correct distinct errors. A full real-browser pass confirmed the reverted unrestricted Coach picker, the new eligibility-filtered "Plan This Station" picker and its live status badges (collapsed-row and block-level), `MyStationBuilderScreen`'s own editor/summary-card/save/attribution/presence rendering, the Home notification card end to end, and `PracticeDetail`'s conditional entry point both present and correctly absent.

### Goals & Insights
- Lives as a sub-toggle inside a team's Plan-equivalent workflow (folded together because "how this team spends its practice time" covered both, and Goals' original placeholder scope had never been built out separately).
- **Internal Overview/Trends/History navigation** (twenty-sixth session, `GoalsSubnav`): same rounded-pill sub-toggle already used for Coach/Org mode and My Drills/Team Libraries, generalized to three options. Overview keeps the existing goal editor, glance view, and (new) Next Practice Guidance; Trends is new (below); History is unchanged except its own detail view now also shows the Practice Execution Scorecard. The selected view is plain component state, so it naturally survives opening/closing a session from History within the same mount.
- **Side margins fixed** (twenty-ninth session): `TeamGoalsRoute` (`App.jsx`) was the only per-team route missing the `16px 16px` horizontal-padding wrapper that Roster/Equipment's routes both use — Goals & Insights only got the outer `.screen` container's 14px, reading noticeably tighter than its sibling tabs across all three subtabs. Added the same wrapper; removed the now-redundant bottom-padding-only div `GoalsScreen.jsx` had instead.
- **Shared attribution math, extracted once** (twenty-sixth session, `supabase/migrations/20260802000000_goal_attribution_shared_helpers.sql`): every new report below reuses the exact category-attribution rules `get_team_goal_report` already established — a multi-tag drill's minutes split evenly across its tags, a station's full `stationDuration` counted per-station (stations run in parallel, never divided), `'break'`-type activities excluded from the denominator entirely. Server-side, four small `security definer` SQL functions (`session_activity_minutes`, `practice_activity_planned_minutes`, `category_minutes_from_rows`, `total_attributable_minutes`, plus a `public.activity_minute_row` composite type) compose into every new RPC; all four have `revoke all ... from public` since they trust the caller's own authorization rather than checking `auth.uid()` themselves — they're internal building blocks, never meant to be called directly, and PostgREST would otherwise expose them as callable RPCs that leak another team's session timing. Client-side, `categoryMinutesForPracticeActivities`/`calculateGoalGapGuidance`/`calculateProjectedGoalImpact`/`classifyDurationVariance`/`summarizeCategoryTrend`/`drillUsageHeatTier` (all in `constants.js`) mirror the same rules in pure JS so Builder can project an *unsaved* draft locally without a round trip per keystroke.
- **Skill-Development Trends** (twenty-sixth session): a new `get_team_goal_trends(p_team_id, p_window_weeks)` RPC returns every category with a currently-saved goal, bucketed into real Monday–Sunday weeks in the *team's own timezone* (never device timezone, DST-safe via the same `AT TIME ZONE` pattern `create_practice_series` already uses), for the team's saved rolling window (1–12 weeks, same cap as the existing report). Each category renders as a `GoalTrendCard`: current target/rolling-planned/rolling-actual percentages (averaged from the same weekly data the chart plots — see the Gotcha below for why this wasn't true in the first draft), a percentage-point variance badge, a compact inline-SVG line chart (`WeeklyTrendChart` — solid Actual line, dashed Planned line, dotted flat Target reference, so the three read apart without relying only on color), and a deterministic plain-language trend summary (`summarizeCategoryTrend`: 3+ usable weeks required, a 3-consecutive-same-direction streak called out first, then latest-vs-earliest movement, falling back to a planned-vs-actual execution-gap message when the movement itself is flat). Empty weeks render as real gaps in the chart, never a misleadingly-connected line. Tapping a card expands a plain weekly table. **Deliberately different from `get_team_goal_report`**: that report's Planned bucket is forward-looking (what's currently scheduled); Trends' Planned bucket is backward-looking instead, computed from each *completed* session's own saved plan via `practice_activity_planned_minutes`, so Planned and Actual always compare the same practice cohort per week — this is a real, intentional divergence between the two RPCs' math, not a bug, and is why Trends never reuses `get_team_goal_report`'s own percentages for its card headlines. Empty states: no goals ("Set team goals to see development trends"), goals but no completed sessions ("Run a practice live to begin building actual-time trends"), both distinguished from the ordinary loading state.
- **Next Practice Guidance** (twenty-sixth session, Overview, `NextPracticeGuidance`): no new RPC — reuses `get_team_goal_report`'s own minute totals (it already returns real minutes, not just rounded percentages, in `planned_minutes`/`actual_minutes`/`denominators`). Uses rolling Actual when usable actual time exists, falls back to rolling Planned (labeled "Based on planned practice time until actual timing is available"), or the bare goal mix when neither exists yet. Duration comes from the next upcoming non-cancelled practice's `scheduledDurationMinutes`; when none exists, falls back to the team's own most recently scheduled practice's duration rather than guessing 60 minutes (this app has no existing default-duration convention, confirmed by grep before writing this) — with no duration at all, shows percentages and priorities only. Shows the three largest below-target categories (goal-mix minutes for the duration, plus minutes needed to fully close the current rolling gap when mathematically possible, capped at "this gap cannot be fully closed in one practice" when it exceeds the practice length) with a "Show all categories" expansion. Categories at/above target never get a negative recommendation. **Each category row expands on tap** (twenty-seventh session, direct feedback, `CategoryGapRow`/`drillsForCategory`): lists the coach's own library drills tagged to that category (name + duration), or "No drills in your library are tagged to {category} yet" when none match — read-only, no add-to-practice action from this screen.
- **Builder library switcher** (twenty-seventh session, direct feedback): the drill section at the bottom of Builder defaults to "My Library" (own drills only) instead of the previous flat merge of every accessible drill (own + org + any peer sharing with the coach, with no way to tell them apart). A dropdown appears only when more than one source exists for the team being built for — the team's own org (if any) and each peer currently sharing with the coach on that team, both derived from what's already in `data.activityLibrary` (already correctly RLS-scoped) via the same per-owner grouping technique `NewLibraryScreen`'s own Explore shelves use. Selecting a drill from another source still only copies it into the practice, never into the coach's own library (unchanged, pre-existing `addAct` behavior).
- **Combined Builder Goal Guidance** (twenty-sixth session, new `src/components/BuilderGoalGuidance.jsx`, `<BuilderGoalGuidance>`): its own sibling section between Practice Details and The Run of Practice (never inside Practice Details' own body), starting collapsed on every entry with a real summary line (e.g. "2 priorities · plan projects closer to goal"). One `get_team_goal_report` fetch per team selection (cached, not refetched per keystroke); every recalculation as the draft changes — add/remove/resize a drill or station, change duration, switch teams — runs locally via `useMemo` over the same pure helpers Next Practice Guidance uses, with **zero additional network calls per edit** (confirmed live: added a drill to an empty draft and watched the projected-impact numbers update instantly, including correctly flagging an *overshoot* — adding too much of one category's time pushed its projected percentage past the target and read as "Farther from goal" even though it moved in the right direction, since the math measures distance-to-target, not direction alone). Three parts when expanded: current priorities (same gap logic as Next Practice Guidance, using this practice's own duration), the current draft mix (minutes per category, untagged minutes surfaced prominently, which target categories have zero planned minutes), and projected rolling impact (current % → projected % per category with a `Closer to goal`/`Farther from goal`/`At goal`/`No change` label). Builder has no Coach/Org-mode awareness anywhere today (a pre-existing gap noted below) — the section doesn't need it to be correct, since `get_team_goal_report`'s own `can_access_team` check already covers a director on an org team with no personal `team_staff` row; the "manage goals" link in the empty-goals state falls back to a personal head-coach check only.
- **Practice Execution Scorecard** (twenty-sixth session, `SessionHistoryDetail`, `PracticeExecutionScorecard`): a new `get_session_execution_scorecard(p_session_id)` RPC, rendered near the top of the session detail, before Skill Minutes/Planned vs. Actual. Deliberately no single overall score — structured measures only (planned/actual duration, planned/logged activity minutes, plan completion count, attendance, other/transition time) plus an activity-execution breakdown classified Extended/Shortened/On Plan/Skipped/Logged-but-not-originally-captured. Execution-unit grain was confirmed against the real logging model before writing this, not assumed: every non-station-block `practice_activity` is one unit keyed by `practice_activity_id`; every individual *station* inside a station block is its own unit keyed by `station_id` — the block container itself is never a loggable unit, so nothing gets marked "skipped" merely because logs live at the station grain. Classification uses a shared 60-second on-plan tolerance (`ON_PLAN_TOLERANCE_SECONDS`/`classifyDurationVariance` in `constants.js`, reused by Drill Insights too, so the two features never disagree about what "on plan" means). "Logged but not originally captured" needed a real signal, not a guess: `adjusted_by`/`adjusted_at` alone can't distinguish a live-captured row that was later corrected from one that never existed during the run at all, since both `adjust_session_activity` and `add_session_activity_row` stamp them the same way — a new `session_activity_log.manually_added` boolean (`20260802020000_session_activity_log_manually_added.sql`), set only by `add_session_activity_row`'s insert, makes this reliably derivable going forward (pre-migration manually-added rows just won't get the special badge, an honest simplification rather than a guessed classification). Refreshes automatically after any manager edit (exclude/restore, adjust a logged row, add a missing one) via the same `refresh()` call site the raw log already used.
- **Drill Performance Insights** (twenty-sixth session, owned-drill shelf only — My Drills in Coach mode, Org Drills in Organization mode; never Team Libraries/peer-shared or Public Library drills): reachable via a "View Drill Insights" ellipsis item, always available on an owned drill regardless of usage, opening an empty state when there's none yet. (The card itself no longer shows a colored heat icon — see the Library section above; direct feedback called it too distracting. `DrillInsightHeatIcon`/the heat-tier math still exist in case a less-prominent surface wants them later.) Two-tier query architecture: `get_drill_insight_summaries(p_library_activity_ids[])` is one batch call per shelf (capped at 300 ids, silently drops any id the caller doesn't own/manage rather than trusting the client, still called even though nothing currently renders its color — the count still drives the Most Frequently Used sort), `get_drill_insights(p_library_activity_id)` fetches full detail only when a coach actually opens one drill — usage counts (4wk/12wk/12mo/all-time), average planned vs. actual duration with a deterministic natural-language read-out, on-plan/extended/shortened percentages, reverse-chron usage history, teams, and recent notes (capped 5, each with a per-note "hide" button added twenty-seventh session — direct feedback wanted to hide one irrelevant note without touching the others, reuses the same `archiveNote` RPC `SessionHistoryDetail` already relies on). Lineage is strictly `library_activity_id`, confirmed through normal activities, stations, templates (a template's own `library_activity_id` copies through into the practice the same way a direct drill add does), and manually-added history rows (which can only target an execution unit already in the plan, so they inherit its lineage automatically) — no fuzzy name matching anywhere, so renaming a drill never breaks its history. A copied drill gets its own new `activity_library.id` via `copyDrillToMyLibrary`, so it starts its own insight history with zero special-casing needed. Every usage/note row is additionally filtered to a team the caller can *currently* access, since a personal drill's history can span teams the coach has since left.
- Coach sets per-skill-tag target percentages (category-grouped picker filtered to the team's sport, sum-over-100 validated). Each category also has a typeable number input alongside its slider now (seventeenth session) — landing on an exact value across several sliders that need to add to 100 was fiddly by drag alone.
- **"Measure over the last N weeks" moved into the glance view's own header** (thirty-first session, direct feedback: it belonged with the report it controls, not the Goals card above it that sets targets): now lives in `GlanceView`'s "Target vs. Planned vs. Actual" title, editable inline for `canManage`, plain text for a read-only viewer. **A real, pre-existing staleness bug found and fixed while making this move**: `updateGoalsWindowWeeks` writes straight to `teams.goals_window_weeks` with no client-side refresh call at all -- the control's own `refreshReport()` call (added this session) fixed the report's percentages, but the top-level `data.teams` cache never got invalidated, so the control's own displayed value (re-derived from `team.goalsWindowWeeks` on every fresh mount) silently reverted to the old number the moment a coach navigated away and back, even though the new value was already saved correctly server-side -- confirmed live by querying the database directly while the on-screen control showed the stale value. `TeamGoalsRoute` → `GoalsScreen` → `GlanceView` now also thread `refreshTeams` down, called alongside `refreshReport` after every save.
- **Save-when-not-100% confusion, root-caused and fixed** (seventeenth session): Save was already disabled unless totals hit exactly 100% (`canSave`), but no `.btn` in the whole app had any `:disabled` CSS at all — a disabled primary button looked pixel-identical to an enabled one, so a coach could tap it, watch nothing happen, and have no idea why. Added a global `.btn:disabled{opacity:.4;cursor:not-allowed;}`. Also added real dirty-tracking (`useBlocker` + `beforeunload`, same pattern Builder already uses) so navigating away with unsaved slider edits now prompts a confirm instead of silently discarding them. Both confirmed live: the confirm dialog fired with the exact right message and blocked navigation; the grayed-out Save button was visibly different at a glance. **Save also grays out again immediately after a successful save** (twenty-seventh session, direct feedback): `canSave` alone (totals=100) stayed true after saving, so the button looked exactly as clickable as before even with nothing left to save — `disabled` now also checks the existing `dirty` flag.
- **`get_team_goal_report`/`set_team_goals` stale-column bug, found and fixed** (seventeenth session, `20260730040000_fix_get_team_goal_report_stale_column.sql`): the live function still referenced `team_goals.skill_tag_id`, a column replaced by `skill_category_id` back on 2026-07-19 (`20260719030000`). A same-day migration (`20260719040000_goals_rpcs_category_level.sql`) already contained the correct `create or replace` definition, but it evidently never took effect live — the exact class of migration-history-vs-live-schema drift this file's Session Log has flagged before (thirteenth session). This broke the "Target vs. Planned vs. Actual" glance view for *every* team, silently, until caught by an actual browser test against production. Fixed by re-applying both functions' correct bodies verbatim; verified via a real (and cleaned-up) throwaway account before calling it done, not just a clean migration push.
- A bullet-chart-style glance view: target tick, planned/actual bars, delta chips (only shown at ≥3 points off), an "untagged" row, an "other/transitions" per-practice average. **"Tag Untagged Drills" button** (twenty-ninth session, `canManage` only, not gated on the untagged-time being "high" — a coach cleaning up a small amount shouldn't have to wait for it to become a problem first): deep-links into the Library's new untagged-only filtered view for the team's own sport, described in the Library section above. **Messaging adapts once every library drill is already tagged** (thirty-first session, direct feedback): the "link drills to the library" nudge stops being actionable advice the moment there's nothing left in the library to tag -- whatever untagged time remains at that point came from activities that were never added from the library at all (typed straight into a practice), which this screen has no way to retroactively fix. `GoalsScreen.jsx` now computes `allDrillsTagged` (same sport/owner-scoped untagged-drill count the Library deep link itself uses) and swaps in a different, accurate message ("Every drill in your library is already tagged...") with the CTA hidden, instead of repeating advice that's already been followed.
- A History section, backed by `get_team_session_history(team_id)`, is the first real frontend read path for `session_activity_log`'s actual (not just planned) timing: reverse-chronological sessions with wall-clock duration, attendance, top-3 skills, and manager-only editing (exclude a session from goals, adjust a logged row's start/end, or log a row that was never captured live).
- **History consolidated: the Schedule Agenda tab's own "Show/Hide Completed / History" section is gone, and Goals & Insights' History detail was rebuilt to look and act like Schedule's history did** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback — scoped literally to "remove history from the schedule agenda view," so Month view's own past-practice access via `DaySheet`/`HistoryViewer` was deliberately left alone, not folded into this rework). `SessionHistoryDetail`'s layout now leads with the practice date + `[Edit/Done Editing][Delete]` header, then Run Now (`runNowFrom` — copies the practice's own activities into a brand-new unscheduled practice via `savePracticeTree(null,{...stripIdsForCopy(activities)})`, then jumps straight into Builder for it) and Save as Template (mirrors `HistoryViewer`'s own inline-name-input flow) as full-bleed primary actions, then Print/Export PDF, then a **Planned vs. Actual** section moved to lead the content (visually resembling Schedule's own history view, per direct feedback), then the Practice Execution Scorecard, Skill Minutes, and Notes. **One edit toggle now gates everything editable**, replacing what used to be a separate Edit affordance on every single Planned-vs-Actual line — the per-log "adjust"/"log actual time" buttons and the notes' delete `×` are all now conditioned on the screen's own `editMode` flag, off by default.
- **Unreviewed-notes now surface actively on Home too, and the History tab itself gets a visible indicator** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback): a new `get_teams_with_unviewed_notes(p_team_ids[])` RPC (plain SQL, `security definer`, joins `practices`→completed `practice_live_sessions`→unarchived/unviewed `notes`, filtered per-row through the existing `can_access_team`) drives a new Home nudge banner — any home team with unviewed notes gets a "review the practice" call to attention. `GoalsSubnav`'s History pill also gained a small red-dot indicator (new this session) reflecting the same `anyUnviewed` signal. The underlying pieces this builds on — per-session mark-viewed-on-open (gated `canManage`, so a read-only viewer glancing at history doesn't clear the head coach's unread state), and the "Mark all as viewed" bulk action inside the `view==="history"` list — already existed correctly from an earlier session and needed no changes, confirmed by reading the code before assuming otherwise.
- **"Delete Practice"** (thirty-first session, direct feedback, top of `SessionHistoryDetail`, `canManage` only, real confirm first): the same soft-delete `archivePractice()` every other "Delete" in the app already uses. `get_team_session_history` needed a matching change to actually make it stick, not just cosmetically -- its `sessions` CTE joined `practice_live_sessions` to `practices` on `team_id`/`status` only, with no `archived_at` check at all, unlike every other practice-listing query in the app, so an archived practice's session would otherwise keep showing up in History forever. Fixed in `20260805010000_session_history_excludes_deleted_practice.sql` (dry-run verified, then a byte-identical-before-edit check via `pg_get_functiondef` per this file's own documented drift-checking convention). Verified live: deleting a seeded completed session immediately dropped it from the list.
- **Logged-row time editing no longer shows a date picker** (thirty-first session, direct feedback -- "I don't think any user will ever need to change the date"): `TimeRangeForm` used two `type="datetime-local"` inputs; now shows time-only Start/End (`type="time"`) plus a Duration (min) field, all three kept mutually consistent (editing Start shifts End by the same amount, holding duration fixed -- a move, not a resize; editing End or Duration recomputes the other, holding Start fixed). The date itself is derived once, invisibly, from the log row's own existing date or (for a brand-new "Log actual time" row that has neither yet) the session's own end date -- never something a coach picks. Same component serves both "adjust an existing row" and "log a row that was never captured live."
- The older, separately-used `HistoryViewer` component (`CommandScreen.jsx`, reused by `ScheduleScreen`/`ManageScreen`) was deliberately left untouched rather than retrofitted with this — it only ever receives a bare `practice` object, no `session_id`, so a new `SessionHistoryDetail` component was built instead to avoid destabilizing three already-working call sites. **It does now show the practice's actual run time, not its scheduled time** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback: running a practice hours or days before its scheduled slot showed the *scheduled* time in history, which read as simply wrong): a new, narrowly-scoped `fetchPracticeActualStart(practiceId)` queries `practice_live_sessions` directly for the most recent `status='completed'` row's `created_at`, deliberately not touching the shared `fetchPracticeRunStatus` helper (whose `{practice_id:"completed"|"started"}` string-map return contract other call sites depend on) — `HistoryViewer`'s header date/time line prefers this actual timestamp over `fmtDate(practice.date)+startTime` whenever a real session exists.
- **`SessionHistoryDetail`'s Notes card** (added on top of the existing `notes` display, which used to just be plain text) now groups notes by drill vs. end-of-practice, shows each note's author + role (or "Helper" + whatever freeform label an anonymous submitter typed) and any @mentioned players as chips, and lets `canManage` archive a note (`archiveNote` — new; the underlying `notes` table had no update policy at all before this session). **Notes now display under the drill they were captured in specifically (not just grouped alongside it), general/end-of-practice notes stay at the end, and per-note delete is gated behind the same `editMode` toggle the rest of the rebuilt History screen uses** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback) — the delete `×` only renders while editing, so a coach browsing history read-only can't accidentally remove a note.
- **Development Pulse** (twenty-eighth session, `src/components/DevelopmentPulseCard.jsx`): a single dynamic CTA card on Home, directly beneath the next-practice hero, Coach mode only (never Org mode — a cross-team director-facing version is explicitly a separate, future widget, not this card reused with a random team). Resolves to exactly one of 7 states via a pure, presentation-neutral `resolveDevelopmentPulseState()` (`constants.js`) — no categorical fifth variation of goal math: it's built entirely from `get_team_goal_report` (extended this session with one new field, see below) plus the same `categoryMinutesForPracticeActivities`/`calculateGoalGapGuidance`/`calculateProjectedGoalImpact` helpers Builder Goal Guidance and Next Practice Guidance already use. States in priority order: no categories for the team's sport → goals not configured → insufficient usable history (`DEVELOPMENT_PULSE_MIN_COMPLETED_SESSIONS=2`) → data quality too weak (`DEVELOPMENT_PULSE_MAX_UNTAGGED_PCT=25`, outranks a category recommendation on purpose) → largest gap missing from the next plan → next plan materially improves the largest gap → a meaningful gap not covered by the previous two → everything aligned (`DEVELOPMENT_PULSE_BALANCED_TOLERANCE_PTS=3`, with its own override: a currently-aligned team whose next plan would move a category materially *farther* from goal falls through to the gap state instead of a false all-clear). A bullet-bar SVG graphic (Actual/Projected/Goal on one 0–100 axis, solid fill + hatched-pattern projected extension + a goal tick, never color-only) — no Recharts, no new charting dependency, same inline-SVG convention as the Trends chart. **Focus-team selection** (`resolveDevelopmentPulseFocusTeamId`) is next-practice's team first, else the home team with the most recently completed session (one new batch RPC, `get_teams_recent_completed_session(p_team_ids[])`, so this never becomes an N+1 per-team query), else the first team. **Spec compliance bug found and fixed during live verification**: the "missing from next plan" state's `Plan {N} minutes` CTA didn't check `closable` the way the sibling "meaningful gap" state already did, so it could recommend more minutes than the practice's own scheduled duration (observed live: "Plan 63 minutes" inside a 45-minute practice) — the spec explicitly prohibits this ("Do not use `Plan N minutes` with a number larger than the practice duration"); fixed to fall back to the same "This gap will take more than one practice to close" / "Review Goal Guidance" copy the closable-gap state already used. **Navigation wiring bug found and fixed**: the CTA into Builder (`kind:"builder_goal_guidance"`) initially called `navigate()` directly, which left `BuilderScreen`'s `useState` initializers (which read `editPracticeId` from context) seeing a stale `null` on their first render — `editPracticeId` is only set a tick later via `BuilderRoute`'s own `useEffect`, by which point those `useState` calls had already locked in "new blank practice" defaults and never re-derive. Every pre-existing entry point into Builder avoids this by calling the existing `goToBuilder()` helper (which sets `editPracticeId` synchronously *before* navigating) instead of a bare `navigate()` — `goToBuilder` was extended with an optional 4th `navState` param (forwarded to `navigate()`'s own `location.state`) so Development Pulse's deep link (`openGoalGuidance`, `highlightedSkillCategoryId`) could go through the same safe path instead of bypassing it. `BuilderGoalGuidance` force-opens on arrival (`startOpen`) and gives the highlighted category a one-time CSS pulse (fades on its own via `animation`, plus a 2.6s timer clears the prop so it doesn't replay on an unrelated re-render) — never permanently changes Builder's own open/collapsed state or dirty-tracking. The "Review Untagged Time" CTA has no precise in-page anchor per spec, so it lands on Goals Overview and gets the same one-time pulse treatment on the Untagged row instead (`emphasizeUntagged` via `location.state`, consumed once by `GoalsScreen`). "View Trends" lands directly on the Trends subtab (`location.state.openGoalsView`). All of this verified live end-to-end with a disposable coach account walked through data-quality → missing-from-next-plan → aligned-with-a-moving-away-override → aligned states via direct data mutation (not 7 separate synthetic teams), plus a 19-case pure-function unit pass (`resolveDevelopmentPulseState`/`resolveDevelopmentPulseFocusTeamId`) covering every state, the tie-breaker (stable sort, first-configured-category wins), and focus-team priority order. Also verified: card absent in Org mode (code-inspection only — `!isOrgMode &&` gate, same pattern as every other mode-gated surface in this file, not re-tested live against a real org account), phone-width layout (375px, no clipping/overflow). **A second, unrelated FK gap found and fixed while cleaning up test data**: `session_activity_log.practice_activity_id`/`.station_id` had no `ON DELETE` action at all (unlike every other link in that same cascade chain, which already cascade), blocking deletion of a team/practice's history the moment any activity had been logged against it — fixed to `ON DELETE CASCADE` (`20260805000000_session_activity_log_activity_cascade.sql`, dry-run verified, applied to production). **An assistant-only coach with zero head-coach teams now sees a "Create a Team" prompt instead of nothing** (forty-fourth session, direct feedback: "their pulse should prompt them to create a team first"): since the card is already scoped to `isHeadCoach` teams only (forty-second session fix), a coach who's only ever an assistant had no focus team and the card simply didn't render, with no explanation. A new prompt card renders in that specific gap (`!isOrgMode && !focusTeam && headCoachTeams.length===0`) with a button to `/teams`.

**Twenty-ninth session addendum — collapsible, persisted, with a new-insight indicator:** the header now has a caret (`Ic_Caret`) that collapses the card down to just its header row; collapsed/expanded state is persisted per-coach (`localStorage`, keyed by `coachId` — this is one physical widget on Home, not a per-team preference, so it stays collapsed across sessions and across whichever team ends up as the focus team on a later visit) rather than per-team or in component state. A small red dot appears next to the header label whenever the card is collapsed *and* the resolved state has changed since the coach last had it open — tracked via a cheap fingerprint (`teamId|state|categoryId`, deliberately excluding percentages/minute counts so ordinary week-to-week wobble in the same underlying situation doesn't re-trigger it) also persisted in `localStorage`; expanding the card (or it simply being expanded when a new state resolves) marks the current fingerprint seen and clears the dot. **A real, pre-existing bug found during this session's live verification, not part of the requested work**: the `insufficient_history` state's goal-mix bar (`GoalMixStrip`) was fed raw `report.skills` rows (`target_pct`, snake_case) while reading `c.targetPct` (camelCase) — every segment computed a `NaN` width, so the bar rendered as nothing at all (invisible, not just wrong), and its `aria-label` read "Passing undefined%, Shooting undefined%". Fixed by mapping to camelCase at the call site, matching every other graphic component's prop convention in this file. Verified live: collapse/expand, persistence across a hard reload, the red dot appearing after a background data change (team goals set via direct SQL while collapsed) and clearing on expand, and the goal-mix bar rendering correctly (both the visual bar and the corrected aria-label) after the fix.

### Live sessions
- **A peace-of-mind sync-confidence badge doubling as a manual refresh (forty-fifth session continued again, direct feedback: "if I notice my screen isn't loading, it would be nice to have a refresh option")**: the header's previously-static "● Live" label is now `SyncBadge`, always tappable, and honestly reflects how current the screen actually is instead of always claiming "Live." `lastSyncedAtRef` is stamped on every real confirmation this client is current with the server -- a realtime message received, a background reconcile poll succeeding, or a write succeeding -- so seconds-since-last-sync is a single answer to "how stale could this possibly be," regardless of which sync path is actually working. Green "Live" under 12s since last sync, amber "Reconnecting…" from 12-25s, red "Refresh" past 25s (purely time-based, not tied to the realtime channel's own reported status, so a harmless brief reconnect blip doesn't flash a warning). Tapping it at any health level does a real `window.location.reload()`, not a soft in-app resync -- deliberate, since this app already re-derives the entire live session (timer, stage, controller) from the server on every fresh mount (see "A coach can leave a live session without ending it" below), so a reload is already a fully correct resync for free, and it's the one thing that still works if the tab's own JS is what's actually stuck, which no in-app retry logic can fix from inside itself. Verified live: joined a real session, confirmed the badge renders correctly (green dot, "Live" label, real DOM button with a "Tap to refresh" title), tapped it, confirmed a real page reload occurred and landed back in the exact same live session with the timer correctly continuing from server state -- validating the "reload is already a correct resync" assumption directly, not just in theory. Not yet verified reaching the amber/red degraded states live (would need an actual multi-minute connectivity gap to trigger); the thresholds (12s/25s, against a 7s poll interval) are a reasoned default, not empirically tuned.
- **Sync no longer relies on Realtime alone (forty-fifth session, direct feedback from the first live multi-device test)**: `CommandScreen`'s `session` state used to update only from a single `subscribeToLiveSession` `postgres_changes` subscription -- Realtime has no catch-up/replay, so any update that happened while a client's websocket was disconnected (a real, common occurrence on weak venue wifi/cellular) was gone for good once it reconnected, with nothing to notice or correct it. This is why two coaches' screens could disagree on the timer, the current drill, or the station rotation, and why "Take Control" from stale state silently no-op'd into a resync on the first tap instead of actually taking control (the version-conflict check failed against a version the client didn't know had already moved) before succeeding on a second tap. `subscribeToLiveSession` (`src/supabase.js`) now takes an optional connection-status callback; `CommandScreen` uses it to force a full reconcile on every reconnect (not just the first), plus a 7-second background poll as a second backstop for a socket that looks connected but silently isn't -- both funnel through one `reconcileSession` that only ever moves state forward, keyed off `version`, and never treats a transient fetch failure as "the session ended." Also hardened `fetchLatestGroups` against throwing outright on a network failure (it previously only handled a clean Postgrest `{error}`) and gave the two live-session effects that depend on it (station-block groupings, per-station sub-groupings -- together, the "Up Next"/rotation view for a station block) a bounded 3-attempt retry, since an unhandled rejection in either one used to leave that data null for the rest of the mount with nothing to retry it, which is what "Up Next in stations went blank" actually was. Not yet re-verified with two real devices under real flaky-network conditions -- confirm at the next live test. **Write side of the same shape, fixed forty-fifth session continued the same day**: `writeSession` used to read `session.version` from whichever render was current when it was called, so two rapid writes (the ±1 min buttons tapped twice quickly, a nudge right after a pause) could race -- the loser rejected as a stale-version conflict, its own optimistic update silently discarded by the resync that follows, visible as the timer jumping forward then snapping back. Every write now funnels through a queue (`writeQueueRef`) so a queued write only executes once every write ahead of it has resolved, reading `sessionRef.current` (kept authoritative the instant a write resolves) instead of a stale closure.
- **Audio cues are more robust to real field conditions (forty-fifth session continued, direct feedback: "the audio even wasn't consistent during the live run")**: two separate real causes, not general flakiness. The "two minutes remaining" cue depended on `elapsed` landing inside a narrow 4-second window on a live tick, but the `setInterval` driving `elapsed` gets throttled or suspended whenever the tab is briefly backgrounded (an app switch, a notification) -- a throttled tick can jump straight over that window and silently skip the cue for the rest of the phase, forever, with no trace. Widened to an at-or-past-threshold check (`rem2<=120&&rem2>0`), the same shape the time's-up buzzer already used. Separately, every sound-file `audio.play()` call swallowed a rejected promise with nothing else happening -- an interrupted audio session (a call, a Bluetooth handoff, autoplay policy re-engaging after backgrounding) meant the time's-up cue's chained `onended -> speak("Time")` never fired at all, going completely silent for the single most consequential cue in the app. Every failure path there (a rejected `play()`, an `onerror`, a missing audio element, a thrown exception) now falls back to speaking "Time" directly instead of going silent. Also added an immediate forced clock tick on `visibilitychange` returning to visible, so a cue that was due while the tab was backgrounded fires the instant the coach comes back instead of waiting out however long is left on a throttled interval.
- **Practice Setup is now a real, shared pre-live stage, not a separate screen (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback from an assistant coach's actual broken experience)**: `practice_live_sessions.setup_confirmed_at` (nullable timestamptz, `20260805040000_start_or_join_live_session.sql`) is null while the session is collaboratively staging — attendance, coach/player station assignment — and gets set once "Start Practice" is tapped, at which point the run actually begins at activity 0. Both the head coach and any assistant now land on the exact same `stage==="attend"` screen (relabeled "Practice Setup," not "Attendance") when either one reaches `/run/:practiceId`, instead of the old flow where a second coach's "Run Practice" tap silently force-started the drill timer out from under the head coach mid-setup, blanked the second coach's own screen, and demoted the head coach to read-only with no visible content. A new atomic RPC, `start_or_join_live_session(p_practice_id)`, replaces the old client-side "check for a session, then insert one" race entirely: it selects for an existing active session first and only inserts inside a nested `begin...exception when unique_violation` block if none exists, backed by a new partial unique index (`practice_live_sessions_one_active_per_practice on (practice_id) where status='active'`) — two coaches racing to open the same never-yet-live practice now deterministically get the same single session row instead of a duplicate, verified live (both via a rolled-back functional RPC test proving `created:true` then `created:false` on repeat calls, and later organically during real repeated "Join Practice" visits, confirmed by a direct DB query showing exactly one session row). Practice Setup itself now handles player *and* coach attendance plus station assignment for both coaches and players in one screen (`StationSetupSection`, new) — tap a station's "Assign Leader," tap a player chip to open a move-picker and reassign them to a different station, all live and shared across every coach viewing setup (backed by the existing `session_groups`/`session_group_members` append-only tables, keyed by session+activity). The assistant's entry-point button is now always labeled "Join Practice" (see the smart-button note below); "Start Practice" only appears once setup is confirmed, and only advances the whole session to the first drill for everyone watching. **Explicitly decided not to add a confirmation prompt before "Take Control"** (direct feedback: "anyone can take control and we now see who has control so I don't think they will be taken off guard... let's leave it for now") — the existing unilateral, no-confirmation Take Control behavior (see below) is unchanged.
- **Abort Practice is now available from Practice Setup itself, not just the live stage (forty-third session)**: `abortPractice` (session `status='abandoned'`, same shared end screen as a real finish, just framed as "Practice Aborted") already existed but was only ever wired into the live stage's own ellipsis menu. Practice Setup's identical menu (`Leave` / `Edit Practice`) had no way to back out of a run at all, including a mistaken "Run as New Practice." Same `isController` gate as the live-stage version.
- **`PreviewView` (the `/preview/:token` screen) no longer demotes a coach to a read-only anonymous `HelperView`** (thirty-second session continued): its polling/refetch logic now checks `can_manage && has_live_session` first and redirects straight to `/run/:practiceId` (the real coach Setup/live screen) before ever falling through to the old `is_live` → `/live/:token` anonymous-viewer redirect, which is exactly the bug the assistant coach hit (their own screen going blank because it redirected into the helper view instead of the real command screen the moment the head coach's tap made the session "live"). `get_preview_view` (`20260805050000_preview_view_setup_stage.sql`) now also returns `has_live_session` alongside a `is_live` that's true only once `setup_confirmed_at` is actually set, so a coach who reaches Preview while another coach is still in Setup gets routed into Setup too, not treated as "not live yet."
- Real-time state machine (`practice_live_sessions`) with take-control/hand-off between coaches, pause/resume, ±1 min nudge, station rotation, append-only attendance (`session_attendance`), and per-activity dwell-time logging (`session_activity_log`).
- **Strict single controller, no exceptions** (confirmed decision, Assistant Coach handoff §1.3): whoever holds control is the only one who can advance, adjust the timer, mark attendance, or end the session — including the head coach; there is no "head coach always can regardless of controller" carve-out. This was already the shipped model (`isController` already gated every action) except for one place: **mid-run plan editing (`LiveEditBuilder`) is gated on role, not control** — an assistant who takes control still can't open it, unlike every other controller action. Fixed this session (`isController && amHeadCoach`, was `isController` alone).
- **Non-controller's action-button area** (where advance/±1min/timer normally render) now shows who's driving ("Coach X is running this") plus a deliberately low-emphasis (outline, not primary) "Take Control" button, in that exact spot rather than a blank area or a bold button — a bold button there risked an accidental tap seizing control from whoever's mid-drill. A brief top banner still shows a passive "Read-only" status; the actual tap target lives only in the controls area now.
- **Toast on control change**: the coach who just lost control gets a brief, non-blocking "Coach X took control" toast, so a habitual tap on Next/±1min a beat later is explained rather than silently doing nothing.
- Offline resilience: single-pending-write retry with backoff, distinguishes "network down" from "someone else moved the session" so a signal drop doesn't blank the screen.
- A coach can leave a live session without ending it — safe because the whole run state (timer, stage) re-derives from `current_phase_started_at` on mount, not from anything held only in memory.
- **"Turn On Audio?" prompt** (seventeenth session): audio defaults off and was easy to never discover via the small header speaker icon alone. A one-time popup now shows the moment any viewer — coach (`CommandScreen`) or anonymous helper (`HelperView`) — lands in a live session (`stage==="live"` for the coach, session-active for the helper), reminding them to turn their device volume up. Fires once per session id per mount (same guard pattern the station-leader warning modal already used); the two modals don't overlap (station warning waits for the audio prompt to be dismissed first).
- **Audio cues** (coach view only): a silent background `<audio>` loop + Screen Wake Lock keep the tab alive when the phone locks; real audio files play alongside or instead of `speechSynthesis` (`tennis-beep.wav` for the fixed 2-minute warning; time's-up is coach-selectable, see below). Time's-up plays the chosen sound, then a spoken **"Time"** once it ends (`audio.onended` triggers `speak("Time")`, reusing the same `speechSynthesis` call already used for "Two minutes remaining" — not baked into the audio file, since that's real browser TTS, not a recording). `HelperView` (parent-facing) is still speech-only — not yet extended with the sound+"Time" treatment.
- **Live Practice Audio settings** (2026-07-30, Settings screen): the coach picks a time's-up sound (Whistle/Buzzer/Ding/Beep — `public/audio/{whistle,gym-buzzer,ding,beep}.wav`, the latter two newly synthesized) and an announcer voice, each with a tap-to-preview. Stored in `localStorage` (`AUDIO_CUES`/`getAudioCuePref`/`getVoiceURIPref`/`resolveVoiceByURI`/`loadVoices`, `constants.js`), not the database — deliberately per-device, since available `speechSynthesis` voices differ entirely by browser/OS and a voice chosen on one device may not exist on another. Voice selection is a **full picker**, not a Male/Female toggle: an initial version guessed gender via a name-based heuristic (the Web Speech API has no real gender metadata) and just took the first match, which surfaced a dated, robotic-sounding voice ("Daniel") ahead of much better ones the heuristic's hint list didn't know about — there's no way to infer voice *quality* from a name. Replaced with a real list of every voice installed on the device (`loadVoices()` handles `getVoices()`'s async population via `voiceschanged`), each with a preview button, remembered by the specific `voiceURI` chosen. **Currently narrowed to just Samantha/Daniel** (`CURATED_VOICE_NAMES` in `SettingsScreen.jsx`) after Jax tried the full device list and found every option "simply awful" except those two — a stopgap until real recorded voice clips replace `speechSynthesis` entirely for the fixed phrases the app speaks (see Known Gaps). Defaults are whistle (cue) and Daniel (voice, via `resolveDefaultVoice()`) whenever no explicit preference is saved -- already correct in the runtime logic as of a session-40-ish fix (`ddb930b`), but the picker's own "no preference chosen" row was still labeled "Device Default," which read as if the real default were something else. Relabeled "Default (Daniel, if available)" forty-second session, and the row's own preview now matches (`resolveDefaultVoice()` instead of finding nothing for an empty `voiceURI`).
- **Notes, captured live**: a "Quick note..." bar during the run and an "End of Practice Notes" textarea on the completion screen (both pre-existing, `createNote`/`notes` table) now support typing `@` to tag a player — a picker filtered to the roster inserts the name into the text and records the tag structurally (`note_player_tags`), surfaced later in Goals & Insights and on `PlayerProfile`. See the Notes section below for the full model.
- **Station leader assignment** (2026-07-29): `stations.team_staff_id`/`helper_name` already existed but had no visibility before a practice started and no safety net once it did. `get_preview_view` now also returns `can_manage` (server-computed off `auth.uid()` via `can_coach_team`, since `/preview/:token` is the same route/component for both a signed-in coach and an anonymous shared link) and, only when true, the team's `team_staff` roster, each station's real `id`, `practice_id`, and the caller's own `my_coach_name` — `PreviewView` uses this to show "No leader assigned -- tap to assign" per station plus a "Share Setup Link" button, reusing `updateStationLead`. Separately, in the live `CommandScreen`, the first time a session reaches `stage==="live"` after attendance, a one-time warning modal lists any station_block stations still lacking a leader, letting the coach assign one, share the live link, or explicitly leave it unmanned. Home's hero presents "Practice Setup" and "Review Plan"/"Start Practice" as co-equal side-by-side buttons for a planned practice (sized `blg` with `minWidth:0` — an earlier version used two `bxl` buttons, which overflowed the viewport on phone-width screens since flex items default to `min-width:auto` and can't shrink below nowrap text's own width). `PreviewView` also gained a "Back to Home" link and a persistent "Start Practice" bar (both `can_manage`-only) — previously a coach reaching Practice Setup from Home had no way back except the browser's own Back button, and no way to actually start the run from that screen.
- **"Who has this open" presence** (2026-07-29): a small live badge on `PreviewView`, the staff `CommandScreen`, and `HelperView` showing which coaches currently have a practice's setup/live view open and how many anonymous viewers there are. Pure Supabase Realtime Presence (`subscribeToPracticePresence`, `src/supabase.js`) — no new table, no RLS, just ephemeral client-to-client state over the same websocket infra `subscribeToLiveSession` already uses (a *public* channel, not the newer "private channel" feature, so no extra Realtime authorization config was needed). Keyed by `practice_id` (not `live_session_id`) specifically so a coach still in Practice Setup and a helper who already opened the live link show up on the *same* channel — required adding `practice_id` to `get_live_session_view`'s output too (it never returned one before). Each client tracks `{kind:'coach',label}` or `{kind:'anon'}`; verified end-to-end with two real anonymous browser tabs on the same preview link — the badge correctly showed "2 people watching" and dropped back to "1" the instant the second tab closed.
- **Stations-intro and transition screens now visually distinct from actual coaching** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback: "this will help a coach know they are transitioning rather than coaching"): the block-intro ("Get everyone to their station") and transition ("Rotate Now") sections both re-skin to a near-black background (`#0d1512`) with `PreviewView`'s existing dark-mode palette (green/gray/white/red text, translucent card borders) instead of the normal light coaching-screen chrome. The intro's redundant "Start Block ▶" button (bottom of the screen, alongside Reshuffle) is gone per direct feedback — the block starts on its own timer; only "Reshuffle" remains, full-width.
- **Live header layout reordered** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback): the drill/activity title is now bigger (22px→30px) and the live-viewer-count `PresenceBadge` moved to sit directly under the "LIVE" row, above the title, with any subtitle below the (now larger) title — previously the subtitle rendered above the title and the presence badge came after it.
- **Player-attendance pill gets a hand icon** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback): a small raised-hand SVG (`Ic.Hand`) now precedes the "x/y" count on both the coach (`CommandScreen`) and helper (`HelperView`) attendance pills, so it reads unambiguously as player attendance rather than some other counter.
- **Zero-total practice components (checklist/water break/etc.) show no "0/0 covered" during live view** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback): the focus-label suffix (" - N/M covered") is simply omitted when a component has zero checklist items, rather than always rendering a count that's meaningless for a water break or a checklist-free intro.
- **"Two minutes remaining" audio skipped for short segments** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback — it read as odd hearing a 2-minute warning at the very start of a 2-minute water break/transition): the cue's own timer effect now bails (`if(phaseSecs<=120)return;`) before its existing `[118,122]`-second trigger window, so any segment 2 minutes or under never fires it at all.
- **Overview toggle gets a caret** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback): the Overview/Close button on the live screen now shows a `Ic.Chev` caret (pointing up while open, meaning "tap to collapse") after its label, on both the coach and helper views, so it visually reads as expandable/collapsible rather than a flat action button.
- **Ellipsis menu cleaned up** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback — "needs a refresh"): Mute Audio/Enable Audio toggle, Share for Attendance, and Restart Practice are all removed from the live-session ellipsis menu (`restartPractice` and its only caller deleted outright); Leave/Edit Practice/Share Live View/End Practice/Abort Practice remain.
- **A rostered coach assigned to a station now defaults straight into that station's own zoomed-in view once stations-intro ends, instead of the shared overview** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback): a new effect fires exactly once as `inBlockIntro` transitions false, looks up the signed-in coach's own `team_staff` id among `cur.stations`, and `setFocusSt`s to it when found — a signed-in coach with no station assignment, or any anonymous shared-link viewer, still lands on the all-stations overview and can tap into a station manually, unchanged.
- **The stations-overview screen supports live manual reassignment, and its redundant "Start Block" button is gone** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback): the all-stations grid's player-chip row now renders the same interactive `PlayerChipLive` (tap to move/view profile) that Practice Setup's own station editor uses, for any `isController` viewer, instead of a read-only chip — writes go through the existing `session_groups`/`saveSessionGroups` pipeline, live for every viewer on the session. The bottom "Start Block ▶" button (redundant with the rotation timer already running) is removed.
- **Upcoming-drill "Up Next" items are clickable for every viewer, not just the controller** (thirty-second session continued, direct feedback): tapping a queued item opens a read-only, grayed-out `UpcomingPreview` modal ("Coming Up · Preview Only") showing that activity's equipment, location, and — for a station block — each station's own equipment/location/assigned players, without advancing the session. `get_live_session_view` (`20260805060000_live_session_upcoming_detail.sql`) now returns `description`/`coaching_points`/`sublocation_name`/`equipment`/a per-station `stations[]` array (name, sublocation, equipment, `player_ids` from `stations.assignments`) on every `upcoming_activities` row, not just name/duration/type. For a station block specifically, the preview prefers any live-reassigned groups already saved for that activity (`fetchLatestGroups`) over the block's own static `assignments`, so a coach previewing a not-yet-started rotation sees the *current* player groupings, not stale planned ones.
- **Station transition/Up Next rotation text clarified to TO:/FROM:, and a negative-duration rendering bug fixed** (forty-fourth session, direct feedback): the transition screen and the Up Next preview both used to show an ambiguous "from X / → Y" sentence — now player names render bold on the first line, followed by a bold-green "TO:" line and a bold-gray "FROM:" line, in `HelperView`, the coach's live "Rotate Now" section, and the Next Transition preview modal. Separately, "the up next... looks like a long skinny empty pill" traced to `(a.stations.length-1)*a.transitionDuration` going negative whenever a station block had fewer stations than the expression assumed, collapsing the badge's own rendered content — guarded with `Math.max(0,...)` at both the Overview jump-list badge and the Up Next queue badge.
- **A station leader now stays on their own drilled-into detail view across a same-block rotation, instead of being bounced to the overview on every transition** (forty-fourth session, direct feedback): `transitionTo` (`CommandScreen.jsx`, the one shared function behind every advance/rotate/transition) was unconditionally clearing `focusSt` on every call. Station identity — including which physical station a coach leads — stays at a fixed array index across a rotation; only `.assignments` remaps. Fixed to only clear `focusSt` when the patch actually contains `current_practice_activity_id` (a real move to a different activity), not on a same-activity rotation.
- **Equipment Needed (Practice Setup + the anonymous preview) grouped by Area instead of listing a coach's name next to each item** (forty-sixth session, direct feedback): a coach getting the gym/field ready cares what to grab for each spot, not who's nominally assigned to bring it. New `groupEquipmentByArea` (`constants.js`, `buildEquipmentNeeded` itself untouched -- still used by the PDF export, out of scope this session) buckets by each drill/station's own Area (`sublocationId`/`sublocation_name`), preserving the order areas first appear in the practice, with anything lacking an Area collected into a trailing "Other" group. Rendered as a CSS grid (`EquipmentByArea`, shared between `PracticeSetupScreen` and `PreviewView`) -- two columns whenever there's more than one area, one column otherwise, so a practice with several station areas doesn't waste the screen width on a single stacked list.
- **The transition ("Rotate Now") screen now leads with a one-line summary of the whole rotation before the detailed per-group cards** (forty-sixth session, direct feedback: "this will help quickly identify rotations each time"): e.g. "Infield to East Cage, East Cage to West Cage, ... Outfield to Infield." Derived purely from the block's own fixed station order/areas (`cur.stations[i]`→`cur.stations[i+1]`, wrapping), not from `rotatedStations`/who's currently occupying which slot -- the sequence is the same every rotation in a block, only the occupants change, so this needed no new data, just a different read of what Builder already saves. Present in both the coach's own live transition screen and `HelperView`'s.
- **A station leader tapping the group headed their way next now sees that drill's Player Focus (skill-tag notes) right on the transition screen, without needing their phone once they're actually at the station** (forty-sixth session, direct feedback): each transition-screen card gets a "Player Focus" button whenever the *destination* station's drill has a tagged skill category (`categoryIdsForLibraryId(nextSt.libraryId)` for the coach view; the already-server-computed `nextSt.skill_tags`/`.player_focus` from `get_live_session_view` for `HelperView`, which had the data plumbed through since the twenty-ninth-session skill-tags-in-helper-view work but never rendered it here) -- opens the same Player Focus modal pattern the plain-drill view already used, but scoped to the specific incoming group's players (`st.assignments`) and the destination drill, via new `transitionFocus` state in both `CommandScreen` and `HelperView`. **Real test-data gotcha hit and worth remembering**: the button first appeared to silently not work in QA testing -- root cause was a `skill_tags` row created via direct SQL that happened to be `scope:'coach'`, owned by a different user than the QA test account, so RLS correctly excluded it from `data.skillTags` while `drill_tags`/`activity_library` (scoped to the *drill's* own ownership, not the tag's) still returned the reference -- a test-fixture mistake, not an app bug, but the exact shape (`tagIds` non-empty, `categoryIdsForLibraryId` empty) is worth recognizing quickly if it recurs: check the referenced tag's own `scope`/`owner_user_id` against the viewing account before assuming the join logic is broken.
- **The transition screen no longer needs a coach to tap Next once its own timer runs out -- it advances itself, silently, straight into the next rotation and that group's timer** (forty-sixth session, direct feedback: "we don't want the coach to have to start the timer for the next section"). A new effect (`CommandScreen.jsx`, placed after `advance` is declared so it can call it) fires exactly once per transition phase (`transitionAdvancedRef`, same one-shot-until-reset shape as the existing `buzzedRef`/`warnedRef` buzzer guards, reset everywhere those already are -- `transitionTo`, practice start, a timer nudge, mid-live Edit Practice's resume) when `isController && isBlock && blockRotate && inTrans && elapsed>=phaseSecs` -- gated on `isController` specifically so only the controlling coach's client ever writes, exactly like every other advance/rotate/jump action in this app already is (`isController` also gates the manual "Next >" button itself); every other viewer (a non-controlling coach's tab, any `HelperView`) simply observes the resulting session update through their existing realtime subscription / 7s reconcile poll / 3s `HelperView` poll, the same as any other phase change -- no client computes or acts on this independently, which is exactly the class of bug this app's own sync-reliability work (see the sync-badge/reconcile entries above) exists to prevent. `advance()`'s own existing branch logic already guarantees correctness here with no new special-casing: a transition (`in_transition:true`) is only ever entered when `stIdx<stations.length-1`, so `advance()`'s "move to the next rotation" branch is always the one that fires, never "move to the next top-level activity." The buzzer that used to sound at a normal phase's zero is explicitly suppressed for this one phase only (`!inTrans` added to the existing buzz condition) since the auto-advance itself is the intended "no sound needed" signal -- every other phase (a station round, block-intro, a plain drill) keeps buzzing and waiting for a manual Next exactly as before. **Confirmed default audio settings already match across every role asked about, no code change needed**: head coach and assistant coach are the literal same `CommandScreen` component instance with no role branching on `audioOn` (both default `false`); `HelperView` -- used identically whether the viewer is a rostered coach's own shared link or a fully anonymous parent, no "on roster" branching exists there either -- also defaults `audioOn` to `false`. Verified live end-to-end (QA Persistent Wolves, a real station block, both the coach view and a second browser tab on the real `/live/:token` helper link watching in parallel): the transition auto-advanced unattended three separate times across two different test practices with no manual Next tap, `HelperView` picked up each resulting rotation change within its poll interval with correctly-rotated player groups, and a normal (non-transition) station round's timer still buzzed and waited for a manual Next as before.
