Added a real "Player Focus" button (gated on the drill actually having a skill tag, same as the coach's own `categoryIdsForLibraryId` gate) opening a read-only modal listing every present player's note for that tag, sourced from the same `player_focus` map `get_live_session_view` already returns. Added a persistent "Up Next" queue row in the body (the coach's view has always had this in addition to its separate "Overview" toggle panel — HelperView only ever had the toggle), including the same spoiler-avoidance the coach's queue already has (a mid-block rotation shows a generic "coming up" placeholder instead of revealing the real next rotation early); tapping an item opens the existing `UpcomingPreview` modal, already shared between both views. Title/timer/description/coaching focus/coach/location/equipment/grouping were all already present in HelperView from earlier sessions' work — this closed the two real gaps (Player Focus, persistent Up Next) rather than a full rewrite.

**Assistant-coach parity, audited rather than rebuilt**: confirmed via a full `isController`/`amHeadCoach` grep across `CommandScreen.jsx` that an assistant coach already gets the exact same view as the head coach today — every gate found was either `isController` (rotates to whoever currently holds control, not fixed to any one role) or the two `amHeadCoach` checks, both specifically on "Edit Practice" (an intentional permission boundary already documented elsewhere in this file, not an oversight). No code change was needed here; this is what "identical view, permission-gated actions" already looks like.

**"Edit Practice" from Practice Setup's ellipsis was a real, separate, bare-bones builder** (`LiveEditBuilder`, its own component living in `CommandScreen.jsx`) that had never been kept in sync with the real Builder screen's own recent work — no drag reordering (manual up/down arrow buttons instead), no library source picker, no equipment-mismatch check, old row header/subtext. Rebuilding it as a real navigation to the actual `/builder/:practiceId` route was considered and rejected: that would unmount `CommandScreen` (and the live-session refs/closures its `onSaveResume` callback relies on to safely reset the session's current-activity pointer after a mid-live edit) before those closures could run. Instead, rebuilt `LiveEditBuilder` in place using the exact same reusable pieces the real Builder screen already uses — `useActivityDnd`/`ActivityDndContext`/`SortableActivityRow` for drag reordering and last-added sticky-pin, the same library-source-picker logic (`librarySources`/`libSource`/`sourceFilteredLib`, `coachDisplayName`), the same equipment-mismatch dialog flow (`findMissingEquipment`/`resolveDrillEquipmentForCoach`/`EquipmentMismatchDialog`), and the same row header/subtext redesign (coach name in the title, grouping/area/equipment in the subtext) — so editing mid-live now looks and behaves like editing anywhere else in the app, without the real risk of navigating away from a live session's own component instance.

**Practice Setup attendance defaults flipped, plus Mark All/Clear All**: attendance used to default to everyone present, which meant tapping *out* every kid who hadn't arrived yet rather than tapping *in* the ones who had. Now defaults to nobody present except the logged-in coach's own coach record (a real, known exception) — players and other coaches get tapped in as they actually show up. Added "Mark All Present"/"Clear All" buttons above both the Players and Coaches grids for the case where everyone genuinely is here and one-by-one tapping is pure friction.

### Thirty-fourth session (2026-08-06) — 13-item direct-feedback batch from testing the groupings-dialog redesign live: manual pre-assignment, Introduce Stations tap-to-reassign, live header/audio/skill-tag polish

Continuing straight off the prior session's pick-up-and-drop groupings redesign, Jax sent another 13-item batch from testing it live. No account-creation tooling in this environment either, so every change was verified by `npm run check` plus reading the actual code paths involved; two of the items needed real schema changes, applied via `supabase db push` after review (no mutating dry-run needed — both are pure additive columns/fields, nothing to roll back against).

**Groupings dialog got its Reshuffle/Group By controls back**: the pick-up-and-drop redesign (previous session) dropped the old Generate-Random/Group-By-Position-or-Hand shortcuts it replaced, and Jax asked for them back alongside the new manual gesture, not instead of it. Added `regenerateSetupGroups` (`CommandScreen.jsx`) — reshuffle does a fresh random split via the existing `assignGroups`; Group By reuses `groupByAttribute` (already imported for Builder's own station config) against *today's* actual present players, not the full roster — and a small "Reshuffle"/"Group By ▾" control pair inside `GroupingsDialog` itself, gated the same way the pick-up-and-drop gesture already is (`isController`, real `session_groups` writes via `saveSessionGroups`).

**Manual pre-assignment for Partners/Groups in Builder** (a real ask, not just a UI tweak): previously a drill's Partners/Groups split was *always* silently randomized fresh once attendance was taken, with no way to plan who'd be paired with whom ahead of time. Added a `group_assignments` jsonb column to both `practice_activities` and `template_activities` (`20260806080000_activity_group_assignments.sql`) — an array of player-id arrays against the full roster (attendance isn't known yet at plan time), mirroring how a station block's own `assignments` column already works. `ActConfig`'s Player Grouping section (`ActivityConfigs.jsx`) grew a new `ManualGroupAssign` sub-component: Generate Random / Clear / Group By Position-or-Hand shortcuts plus a per-group tap-to-toggle roster (green=here, yellow=in another group, gray=unassigned) — deliberately the same visual language Station's own player-assignment pills already use, not a new pattern. Left untouched (empty `group_assignments`), behavior is unchanged. Practice Setup's own group-seeding effect (`CommandScreen.jsx`) now checks for a non-empty manual assignment first, filtered down to whoever's actually present, before falling back to a fresh random split.

**Builder polish, four smaller items**: (1) the station drill-picker popup's bottom option was hidden behind the tab bar — added `calc(20px + var(--tab) + env(safe-area-inset-bottom,0px))` bottom padding, matching how the rest of the app already accounts for the tab bar. (2) The library source picker showed a vague "A coach's Library" instead of naming anyone — `librarySources` (`App.jsx`) now resolves real names via a new `coachDisplayName`/`coachNameByUserId` lookup (mirroring `NewLibraryScreen.jsx`'s own cross-team name resolution) and adds a genuine "Public Library" source option (which `StationConfig`'s own `filteredLibrary` in `ActivityConfigs.jsx` was separately, redundantly re-excluding via a leftover `!a.sourceCatalogId` filter — removed, or the new source would have always shown empty results). (3) A real regression-of-a-regression: an earlier session's fix for "the last-added drill should stay pinned at the top" had grown a `scrollToRow`/`scrollIntoView` call, which Jax reported *also* felt wrong ("tapping a drill slides the screen up... I shouldn't change locations in my library") — removed `scrollToRow` and its three call sites entirely, relying solely on the pre-existing `position:sticky` mechanism (`SortableActivityRow`'s `sticky` prop), which pins the new row without moving the coach's own scroll position in the library below it. (4) Practice Setup's Run of Practice rows now show a "Coach" label ahead of the leader pill/name, so an unassigned drill reads as "Coach: Unassigned" rather than just a bare, ambiguous pill.

**Builder's Run of Practice row header/subtext redesigned**: per feedback that duration was already visible in the green badge and didn't need repeating, a plain (non-station) row's subtext now shows grouping type (Whole Team/Partners/N Groups) + area + equipment instead — exactly what a coach needs to spot something forgotten at a glance — and the row's title line grew an inline `· {coach name}` so who's leading is visible without expanding. Station blocks were left as-is (their own drill-list-plus-duration-math subtext already serves a different purpose and wasn't reported broken).

**Turn On Audio prompt**: Jax asked whether device volume level is readable from the web, to only show a volume reminder below some threshold. It isn't — no Web Audio/media API exposes a page to the OS or hardware output volume (only in-page gain, which is a different thing entirely) — so there's no way to conditionally suppress the reminder. Rather than leave it folded into the existing paragraph, split it into its own bolded, amber-callout line so it can't be skimmed past, with a comment explaining why it's unconditional.

**Coach name added to the live "Up Next" station preview**: tapping a station block in the queue showed drill name and area but not who was leading it. The controller's own local-plan-based preview builder (`CommandScreen.jsx`) now resolves it via the existing `leaderLabel` helper; the anonymous/shared-link path needed a real schema fix, since `get_live_session_view`'s `upcoming_activities[].stations[]` never selected `coach_name` at all (unlike the *current*-activity version of the same function, which already did) — added via `20260806090000_live_session_upcoming_station_coach.sql`, same `team_staff` join and helper-name fallback as the existing current-activity query.

**"Introduce Stations" (renamed from "Introducing Stations") gained the same tap-to-reassign as the groupings dialog, and player movement moved out of the live per-station view entirely**: these two items turned out to be the same code path from opposite ends, so they were done together. Previously, tapping a player *during* an actual live station opened a list-based "move to which station?" picker (`onClick`), and a 500ms long-press opened their player card (focus areas/notes) — the exact awkward-list-picker UX already replaced in the groupings dialog, still living here untouched, plus a long-press gesture Jax explicitly asked to retire now that movement has a better home. Simplified `PlayerChipLive` to a single tap → player card (no more long-press timers, no more `onMove`); removed the old list-based move modal and its "Move to Another Station" button from the live per-station view entirely. That freed up `movePlayer`/`setMovePlayer`/`movePlayerToStation` (previously that modal's own backend) to be reused, unchanged, inside the "Introduce Stations" block-intro screen instead — tapping a player there now highlights them in place and every other station grows a dashed "+ Drop here" landing zone, identical gesture and visual language to the groupings dialog's own pick-up-and-drop, reusing `movePlayerToStation`'s existing rotation-offset math (still 0 at this point in a block, before any rotation has happened).

**Live header restructured to fix a real layout bug, not just crowding**: the pulsing "Live" dot (`.live`, a 7×7px circle) had gone oval — root cause was a missing `flex-shrink:0`, so a cramped flex row (dot + label + schedule badge sharing space against the entire button cluster via `justify-content:space-between`) could shrink the dot's width below its fixed height under real screen widths. Fixed at the source (`.live` in `App.jsx`) rather than just in one call site. Separately restructured both the controller's and the helper's own header (`CommandScreen.jsx`) so the dot/Live label/schedule badge/presence-badge now sit on their own row below the button cluster, instead of competing with it — giving the ellipsis (and every other header button) the full row width Jax asked for, and directly answering the "move the dot to its own line, followed by who's watching" ask in the same change.

**Untagged-skill reminder in live view**: a drill with no skill tag silently lost its "Player Focus" callout with no indication why or how to fix it mid-practice. Added a reminder note (only for a drill the coach actually owns in their own library — checked via `ownerUserId`/`sourceCatalogId`, since editing someone else's org/peer/public-catalog drill from live view isn't safe) that opens a tag picker on tap; saving calls the existing `updateDrill` (passing through the drill's own unchanged fields alongside the new `skillTagIds`, since `updateDrill` always writes every field it's given) and `refreshLibrary()`, so the change is real and persists for next time, not just a session-local override.



Jax reported testing the app live and hitting a real bug: an August 5th practice, actually run a day early (August 4th) while testing, silently read as "completed" against its own real scheduled slot instead of staying scheduled — plus 12 more items spanning Library, Home, Practice Setup, live-session UX, and Goals & Insights. This session did **not** have access to the account-creation/auth-session tooling this project's own Working Conventions call for (minting a disposable test account is out of bounds for this environment specifically, not a project-convention gap) — every change below was verified by build check (`npm run check`) plus direct **read-only** SQL against the linked production database (`supabase db query --linked --file`, never a mutating query outside the one real migration below) rather than live browser click-through. Flagging this plainly rather than claiming a live-verified "done": these changes should get a real in-app pass before being trusted the way this file's other entries are.

**The early-start bug, root-caused and fixed**: nothing in the app gated *when* a practice could be started — Home's hero, Practice Detail's "Run Now," and Builder's Run Now (1-hour gate only) all just started whichever practice `practice.id` pointed at, however far off its own scheduled time was. Fixed with a shared 2-hour rule (`isMoreThanTwoHoursAway`/`practiceScheduledMs`, `constants.js`, built on `supabase.js`'s existing `teamLocalToScheduledAt` rather than a second date-math implementation) and a new shared 3-choice `FuturePracticeGuardModal` (its own leaf component, `src/components/FuturePracticeGuardModal.jsx` — kept out of both Home/PracticeDetail to avoid a circular import between the two, which already import each other). Home's hero and Practice Detail's own Run Now both now run directly when the practice is live/soon/already-past (missed), and otherwise open the guard: **Cancel**, **Run This Plan as a New Practice** (the existing `runAgainFrom`/`runNowFrom` copy-into-a-fresh-unscheduled-practice pattern — `stripIdsForCopy` + `savePracticeTree(null,...)` — so the original scheduled slot is never touched), or **Run This Practice Now** (the direct, pre-existing path, for a coach genuinely on-site early). Practice Detail's button intentionally was **not** deleted outright despite the literal ask ("remove run now option from the review plan screen") — a missed (already-past, never-run) practice has no other UI path to re-run today (Home's hero only ever surfaces `p.date>=todayStr`, and History only lists practices that actually got a session), so removing it outright would have silently broken re-running a missed plan; gating it the same way Home's hero now is preserves that path while closing the actual reported loophole. Builder's own Run Now keeps its prior, separate 1-hour disable+tooltip (not migrated to this same guard/popup this session — a deliberate, previously-shipped decision, not something reported broken).

**Home's hero button/copy simplified**: "Practice Setup" as a distinct label is gone (direct feedback: it showed for a practice over a week away, where jumping into pre-live setup makes no sense) — always "Review Plan" + either "Join Practice" (a session already exists) or "Start Practice →" (direct if soon/live, the guard above otherwise). No change was needed for "upcoming practices shouldn't show a practice already completed from its own scheduled slot" — `agendaWindow`'s existing `!ran(p)` filter already did this correctly; the reported symptom was entirely the early-start bug above (the Aug-5 slot really did get completed, a day early), not a separate filtering gap.

**A real, separate "glitchy Home" bug found and fixed, not previously reported as this specific mechanism**: the Home hero and Upcoming Practices list could visibly show a practice for a moment and then have it disappear (or the hero could jump to a different practice) a beat after mount. Root cause: `runStatus` starts `{}`, so `ran(p)` reads false for *everything* until `fetchPracticeRunStatus`'s own async round trip resolves — the very first render treated an already-completed practice as still upcoming, then recomputed and dropped it once the real statuses landed. Fixed with a `runStatusLoaded` gate (same shape as this file's existing `hasCompleted`/`checklistDone` fix) — the hero/Upcoming Practices section shows a plain "Loading..." card instead of a guessed-then-corrected one until the first fetch resolves, and is never re-hidden on a later background refresh. A second instance of the same class of bug: Development Pulse's focus-team resolution has a documented "guess `homeTeams[0]`, correct once `recentSessionByTeamId` arrives" fallback for a coach with no next practice and more than one team — which meant the card could render one team's data for a moment and then visibly swap to a different team's. Fixed by withholding the card (`focusTeamDataReady`) until that fetch resolves, but only when it's actually going to run (a coach with a next practice or only one team gets the right team on the first render already, no wait needed).

**Save as Template losing a station block's contents, root-caused (not template-specific)**: read-only SQL against production confirmed a real practice with a `station_blocks` row saved correctly (duration/rotate intact) but *zero* `stations` rows underneath it — the template built from it faithfully copied that already-broken practice, it didn't lose data during templating. The actual defect: `saveActivityTree` (`supabase.js`, shared by `savePracticeTree`/`saveTemplateTree`) logged-and-continued past any insert/update failure without ever surfacing it to the caller — a station-row insert that silently failed (RLS/FK/whatever the original trigger was) still left the surrounding UI reporting "Saved"/"Saved as Template" with data quietly missing, the same root-cause shape this file's very first Gotcha already documents for drill/equipment tag sync. Fixed by collecting every failure and returning `{errors}`, which `savePracticeTree`/`saveTemplateTree` now surface as `{error}` to their existing callers (`handleSaveAsTpl` in both `GoalsScreen.jsx`/`CommandScreen.jsx` already had `if(error){setTplError(...)}` in place, previously unreachable dead code). Historical data for the one broken practice found isn't recoverable (its source stations were already gone, and both affected rows were disposable test/QA data, not the real coach's) — this fix prevents recurrence and, for the first time, tells a coach when a save partially failed instead of a silent false "Saved."

**Practice Setup rebuilt as a black, information-rich "Run of Practice" rundown** (`PracticeSetupScreen`, new, `CommandScreen.jsx`): the thirty-second session's rework made this a real shared collaborative pre-live stage but dropped the informational richness the old public `/preview/:token` view (`PreviewView`) already had. This session merges the two: dark palette (`#0d1512`/`#52b788`, matching `PreviewView`'s own), a live countdown to the real scheduled start (`teamLocalToScheduledAt`, exported from `supabase.js` for this), and every activity — not just stations — listed under a **"Run of Practice"** header (renamed from "Run Order" here and in `PreviewView` itself) with location/equipment/coaching-points per activity and per-station, equipment flagged "not acquired" the same way `PreviewView` already does. `StationSetupSection` (station leader assignment, live player-move) gained the same equipment/location display rather than being replaced — its collaborative editing is unchanged, just richer. The old plain-attendance `AttendanceScreen` is now used **only** for the mid-practice "Attendance" quick-update modal (`isUpdate`); the fresh pre-live case always renders the new dark screen.

**Live-session header layout fixed** (`CommandScreen.jsx`, both the coach view and `HelperView`): the drill title used to be squeezed into a narrow left column alongside a separate button column (`.cc-header`'s own `justify-content:space-between` on two side-by-side children), which is why it wrapped awkwardly instead of spanning the screen. `.cc-header` is now a column: a top row (LIVE/on-time badge on the left, attendance pill + Overview + audio + ellipsis all on the right, all on one line) followed by the presence badge and then the title, now full-width and bigger (30px→34px). The attendance-pill icon changed from a raised hand (`Ic.Hand`) to a plain person silhouette (`Ic.Person`) per direct feedback that a hand-raise read oddly for a bare headcount. The audio toggle's emoji and "On"/"Off" text are now explicit sibling spans in a flex row with a controlled font size, instead of one string — the emoji glyph's own larger rendered size was visually overlapping the word next to it.

**Insights' false "untagged drills" complaint, root-caused and fixed**: non-station Practice Components (Intro/Closer/Checklist/Water Break/Stretch/Other — always saved as `practice_activities.type='checklist'`, never linked to a real library drill) were counted in every category-attribution denominator, both inflating "untagged" and understating every real category's percentage. `practice_activities.type='break'` was already excluded everywhere for exactly this reason but never actually reachable from the Builder UI (every component type writes `'checklist'`, not `'break'`) — extended the same exclusion to `'checklist'` too, both in the shared attribution helpers (`category_minutes_from_rows`/`total_attributable_minutes`, `20260805080000_exclude_admin_components_from_attribution.sql` — Trends/Scorecard/Drill Insights all compose on these and needed no changes of their own) and in `get_team_goal_report`'s own inline CTEs (copied verbatim from its current live definition per this file's own migration-drift-checking convention, then edited). Verified the fix directly against the linked database (`select total_attributable_minutes(...)` with a synthetic `checklist`/`break`/`activity` row triplet correctly returning only the `activity` row's minutes) before and after `db push`. Client-side mirror `categoryMinutesForPracticeActivities` (`constants.js`, used by Builder's own goal-guidance projection) updated to match. Development Pulse's "data quality" state (`resolveDevelopmentPulseState`) additionally gained the same `allDrillsTagged` carve-out `GlanceView` already had — even after the denominator fix, some genuine untagged time can remain from activities typed straight into a practice rather than added from the library, and the card now falls through to a real category recommendation instead of a false "go tag your drills" complaint once there's nothing left in the library to tag.

**Builder's station "Choose from Library" picker now shows and defaults to "My Library"**: it silently merged every accessible drill (own + org + any peer sharing with the coach) with no indication of source — the exact gap the main "My Drill Library" section's own `librarySources`/`libSource` switcher (twenty-seventh session) already closed for the bottom-of-screen add-drill flow, just never threaded into `StationConfig`. `BuilderScreen` now passes its already-scoped `sourceFilteredLib` (not the raw `data.activityLibrary`) into `StationConfig`, plus the switcher's own `librarySources`/`libSource`/`setLibSource`, so the station picker shares one library-source selection with the rest of Builder instead of a second, independent (or missing) one. `ActConfig` (a plain activity's own inline picker, if it ever gains one) has the identical gap and was deliberately left alone — out of the scope Jax actually asked for ("when building a station").

**Library's blue sport-header text, root-caused**: three of the four collapsible per-sport section headers (Equipment, Player Gear, Skill Tags tabs) already set an explicit `color:"var(--black)"`; the fourth (My Drills/Team Libraries) had no `color` set at all on that span, falling back to whatever default the browser/OS supplied (a blue tint on at least one real device). Fixed by setting all four explicitly to `var(--green)` — matching the app's actual "consistent with the rest of the app" convention (most section labels/actives already use the green, not black) rather than just patching the one outlier to match its three siblings' black.

Known, deliberate scope decisions from this session, not oversights: Builder's Run Now keeps its separate 1-hour gate (see above); `ActConfig`'s library picker wasn't touched (see above); the assistant-coach/head-coach "Join" semantics from the spec were confirmed already correct in the existing code (`isSessionLive` is role-agnostic — any signed-in coach sees "Join Practice" the instant a session exists, regardless of who started it or of `canManage`) and needed no change.

### Thirty-third session continued (2026-08-05) — 13 more direct-feedback items from testing the above live, headlined by two real RLS gaps found and fixed

Jax reported the previous batch's own Save-as-Template fix now surfacing a real, reproducible red error, plus a delegated assistant coach unable to schedule or save-as-template at all with no error shown either way. Both were root-caused via direct, rolled-back SQL against the linked production database (never a live account, still off-limits this session) rather than guessed at.

**Two real RLS gaps found and fixed, both about equipment/location a coach didn't personally create**: (1) `practices_insert_manage`/`practices_update_manage` required `can_access_location` for a scheduled practice's location — a delegated planner (`team_staff.can_build_practices`) could pass the team check but still get silently rejected scheduling at the team's own regular location, since that's routinely owned personally by the head coach, not the delegate. Confirmed live (`can_access_location` false, `can_manage_team` false, `can_build_practice_for_team` true, for a real delegated assistant and a real team location) and fixed with a new `can_use_location_for_team` function, extending both policies. (2) `can_link_asset_to_template_activity`/`_station` (and the practice/station equivalents) never accounted for equipment owned by a *teammate* rather than the caller or an org — reproduced live end-to-end (an assistant saving a real practice as a template hit an actual `new row violates row-level security policy` error trying to link the head coach's own gear) and fixed by extending all four functions with a "owned by any non-archived team_staff member of this team" fallback, mirroring the exact reasoning already used for `ast.team_id = tm.id` team-owned assets. A third, separate bug in the same area: `saveActivityTree`'s two join-table helpers (`replaceEquipment`/`replaceChecklistItems`) never checked `.error` on their own inserts at all — even after the RLS fixes, a genuinely failed equipment link would still vanish silently; both now return `{error}` and `saveActivityTree` surfaces it. A fourth, unrelated bug found via the same investigation: Builder's own `lastLocForTeam` fallback (no prior practice for this team yet) defaulted to `data.locations[0]` — the first location in the coach's entire cross-team list by pure array order, not anything to do with the team being built for — silently breaking Run Now/Save for any team whose own location wasn't coincidentally first; now prefers a location the coach actually owns.

**Practice Setup screen taken further, informed by feedback on the redesign from the earlier session today**: attendance is now collapsible (header reads "x/y players present," defaults open) with shorter/more-compact tiles so more of a roster fits on screen; the base `.att-circle` unchecked state's background referenced `--b2`, a CSS variable never actually defined in `:root` — a real, separate bug, not a removed element — so it rendered fully transparent/invisible rather than a visible blank circle, now fixed to a real color with dark-mode and compact variants. Added a first "Equipment Needed" section (aggregated across the whole practice) ahead of the Run of Practice breakdown, plus explicit "use this time to get everyone and everything set up" copy. Run of Practice is now one real ordered list (station blocks interleaved in their actual position, not dumped into a separate section afterward) — each row shows a clickable coach-leader pill ("Unassigned" or the name, tapping opens the same assign-a-coach-or-non-roster-helper picker now shared between drills and stations, the latter needing a new `practice_activities.helper_name` column since only stations had one before), location, equipment (yellow pills), duration, and an expand-on-title-tap for description/coaching focus. Station blocks show equipment/location/leader per station but deliberately never player assignments here anymore (that's what the new optional "View Groupings" button/dialog is for — a read-only preview computed from *today's* actual checked-in attendance, shown in red when it's uneven, never persisted). Added a visible "Share Setup Link" button and a live-view-style ellipsis (Leave/Edit Practice); replaced the old Rebalance-vs-Keep-Groups bottom choice with a single "Run Practice" button, since grouping review now lives per-drill/per-block above.

**Practice plan authorship**: `practices.created_by`/`last_edited_by` (new columns, set once at insert / on every save) threaded through `savePracticeTree`'s ~9 call sites across Builder, Home, Goals & Insights, Schedule, Library, and the live Edit-Practice path; shown on Practice Detail ("Planned by X" / "Last edited by Y" when they differ) and in the live Overview panel.

**Builder's station player/coach defaults reworked**: a new station block starts with zero player assignments (previously every present player was split across stations the instant the block was added) and no default coach on Station 1 (previously auto-set to the head coach) — both now genuinely optional, filled only by Generate Random, a manual pick, or a grouping choice. `seedAllStationGroups` (the live-start seeding function) now detects a station block that was *never* touched at all (every station still has zero assignments) and gives it the same even-random split `rebalance` already does, so nothing silently starts empty once the practice is actually live and attendance is known. Station coach assignment is now a pill row, not a plain dropdown — green for this station, white/plain for available, yellow (with the other station's name) for already assigned elsewhere in the same block; tapping a yellow pill *moves* that coach rather than creating a second assignment, which is what actually prevents the double-booking the old independent-per-station dropdowns allowed. The station "Choose from Library" picker is now a full-screen popup (same overlay pattern used elsewhere) instead of a 220px-tall inline box. Per-station sublocation picker was already present and reactive to the practice's own selected location (confirmed by reading the code, not assumed) — if it isn't appearing, the location likely just has no sublocations configured yet, not a missing feature.

**Also this session**: a "Group by Skill" sort mode added to Library's Drills tab (global skill tag as section header, a multi-tagged drill listed under every one of its tags, an "Untagged" bucket at the end) and the same sort plus a skill-tag filter added to Builder's own library section, which previously had neither. The redundant "`X`min" line on a Builder library row (duplicating the green duration pill already shown) removed. A real regression found and fixed: newly-added drills used to auto-scroll into view (the sticky-pin mechanism itself was still intact) but nothing actually triggered that scroll when adding *from the library* specifically (only the "Done editing" collapse path did) — a coach scrolled down in the library section never saw anything change. Builder's Run Now now surfaces a visible error instead of silently doing nothing if a save genuinely fails.

**Thirty-third session, third pass, same day — the groupings dialog just shipped above had two real bugs of its own, caught immediately on first use**: (1) contrast — every dark-screen `.btn.ghost`/`.btn.outline` button in Practice Setup and its modals (View Groupings, Close, Unassign, Cancel, the coach-name picker buttons) set `color:"#fff"` but never overrode the class's own default background (`.ghost` is `var(--s2)`, a light gray; `.outline` is literally `#fff`) — white text on a near-white background, unreadable. All nine instances fixed with an explicit `background:"transparent"`. (2) The dialog "constantly cycled" the groupings for a plain drill's own Partners/Groups split -- root cause: `computeGroupingsPreview` called `assignGroups` (an internal random shuffle) *inline during render*, and the parent re-renders every second (the countdown's own `setNow` tick) -- every tick reshuffled again, indistinguishable from someone mashing a shuffle button. Fixed at the source rather than papering over it with a memo: extended the exact fetch-or-seed-once pattern a station block's rotation groups already used (and a plain activity's own live in-session split already used once actually running) into Practice Setup itself, for *any* non-station activity with `grouping!=="whole"` — generated once, saved via `saveSessionGroups` immediately, and every render after that (and every dialog reopen) just reads the same stable, real row. This also directly enabled the requested "add player grouping management" — since groupings are now real persisted session_groups rows instead of a one-off render-time computation, a generalized `moveSetupGroupPlayer` (the old per-station-block `moveSetupPlayer`, removed earlier today when Practice Setup stopped showing players inline, brought back in a form that works for both a station block's rotation *and* a plain drill's partners/groups) backs a real tap-to-move picker inside the dialog itself, gated on `isController` same as every other live-session edit.

**Fourth pass, same day — the tap-to-move picker's first cut (tap a player, then choose a destination from a bare list of group labels) was itself an awkward way to move someone with no idea who they'd be joining.** Redesigned as pick-up-and-drop: tapping a player highlights them in place (amber ring, "· picked up") instead of opening a second screen; every *other* group then grows a dashed "+ Drop here" landing-zone slot alongside its real players, so the coach can see the destination's actual roster before committing, not just its label. Dropping is one tap on that dashed slot. Rebalancing a pairing afterward needed no new mechanism at all — it's the exact same pick-up-and-drop gesture run again in reverse (pick up someone from the now-larger group, drop them into the now-short group's own landing zone), since any player in any group can always be picked up and any other group always shows a landing zone.

This session's changes have not yet been live-verified in a browser either — same standing caveat as the prior entry today, and worth a real click-through pass before trusting it the way this file's older entries can be.

### Thirty-second session continued (2026-08-04) — 17-item live-session/History batch, led by a real Practice Setup architecture rework from an assistant coach's broken experience

Jax reported a genuine broken experience as an assistant coach: opening a team's schedule while the head coach was in practice setup, there was no way to get into setup themselves; tapping "Run Practice" force-started the head coach's session out from under them (their own screen went blank white), and the head coach was demoted to read-only, seeing "someone is in control" with nothing rendered. This, plus 16 more direct-feedback items from using the live app, were split into 19 tracked items (#45-63): a new shared "Practice Setup" stage (attendance, player/coach station assignment, collaborative, no drill running yet) that both coaches land on identically, with "Start Practice" as the only thing that actually begins the run; a hand icon on the live attendance pill; blank instead of "0/0 covered" for zero-item components; skip the "two minutes remaining" cue for segments ≤2 minutes; a caret on the Overview toggle; the Library sort control restyled to match the filter row; a leaner live ellipsis menu (mute audio, share-for-attendance, and restart-practice all removed); a distinct dark color scheme for stations-intro/transition vs. actual coaching; a bigger live title with the viewer-presence badge moved above it; a rostered coach defaulting into their own station after intro (anonymous viewers still default to overview); interactive player reassignment directly on the stations-overview screen (and removal of a redundant "Start Block" button there); Home's hero no longer implying "nothing scheduled" when cancelled-only practices exist; upcoming-drill preview made clickable for every viewer, not just the controller; a fix for the Getting-Started checklist flashing then vanishing on every Home load; consolidating history (removing it from the Schedule Agenda tab, rebuilding Goals & Insights' History view in that same look-and-feel, with Run Now/Save-as-Template/PDF/Delete-with-confirmation and Planned-vs-Actual leading); an unread-notes indicator (History tab dot, Home nudge, mark-all-as-read) with notes grouped under their drill and deletable only in edit mode; and history showing a practice's actual run time instead of its scheduled time when run early/late.

Before writing any code for the Practice Setup rework, four architecture questions went to Jax via `AskUserQuestion` rather than guessing: where Practice Setup should live (inside the real live session vs. a separate pre-session screen — chose "inside," the recommended option, which is why `setup_confirmed_at` lives on `practice_live_sessions` itself rather than a new table); how long a manual player-station reassignment during setup should persist (today only vs. saved as a new default — chose "today only," matching the existing append-only `session_groups` semantics); whether Join Practice should be one smart button or two separate ones (chose "one smart button," which is why every Run Now entry point now checks `findActiveLiveSession` and relabels itself rather than adding a parallel UI); and whether "Take Control" should gain a confirmation step now that setup is more collaborative (Jax explicitly declined: "anyone can take control and we now see who has control so I don't think they will be taken off guard... let's leave it for now" — a real answered question, not a default assumption).

**Built, the Practice Setup core:** `start_or_join_live_session(p_practice_id)` (`20260805040000_start_or_join_live_session.sql`) replaces the old client-side check-then-insert with a single atomic RPC — select for an existing active session first, insert inside a nested `begin...exception when unique_violation` block only if none exists, backed by a new partial unique index so two coaches racing to start the same practice deterministically converge on one row. `CommandScreen.jsx`'s mount effect now always calls this RPC and branches on the returned session's `setup_confirmed_at`: null routes to the renamed, expanded `AttendanceScreen` ("Practice Setup," not "Attendance," now also rendering a new `StationSetupSection` for per-station leader assignment and live player-chip reassignment via a move-picker modal); set routes straight to the live drill view as before. `handleAttConfirm` stamps `setup_confirmed_at` and only then does the session become genuinely live. `PreviewView`'s own redirect logic (`get_preview_view`, `20260805050000_preview_view_setup_stage.sql`, now also returning `has_live_session`) was fixed to check `can_manage && has_live_session` before its old `is_live` check, so a coach reaching `/preview/:token` while a session exists — setup or live — always lands in the real `/run/:practiceId` command screen, never the anonymous `HelperView`, which is the direct fix for the reported blank-screen/read-only bug.

**Built, the smaller live-view and Home items** (full detail in the Feature Inventory's Live Sessions/Planning & Scheduling/Library sections above, not repeated here): the hand-icon attendance pill, blank-vs-"0/0" checklist labels, the short-segment audio-cue skip, the Overview caret, the restyled Library sort control, the trimmed ellipsis menu, the dark stations-intro/transition color scheme with the redundant Start Block button removed, the reordered/enlarged live header, rostered-coach auto-focus into their own station, interactive stations-overview reassignment, the clickable grayed-out `UpcomingPreview` modal for upcoming drills (backed by a `get_live_session_view` extension, `20260805060000_live_session_upcoming_detail.sql`, returning full equipment/location/station detail instead of just name/duration), the Home hero's cancelled-practice fallback card, and the `GettingStartedCard` flash fix (`hasCompleted` initial state `null` instead of `false`, so the loading state and "genuinely incomplete" are no longer indistinguishable on first paint).

**Built, History consolidation:** the Schedule Agenda tab's own history section (and its `showPast` state) is removed outright — read literally, this only meant the Agenda tab; Month view's separate `HistoryViewer`/`DaySheet` path was deliberately left as-is, not folded in, since unifying it would have meant making `SessionHistoryDetail` reachable outside its team-scoped Goals & Insights context, a larger refactor the literal request didn't ask for. `SessionHistoryDetail` was restructured to visually resemble Schedule's own history styling: header with Edit/Delete, then Run Now (copies the practice via `savePracticeTree`+`stripIdsForCopy` into a new unscheduled practice and jumps into Builder) and Save as Template as full-bleed primary actions, then Print/PDF, then Planned vs. Actual moved to lead the content, then the Scorecard/Skill Minutes/Notes. A single `editMode` toggle now gates every previously-always-visible per-line edit affordance (adjust/log-time buttons, note deletion) instead of each having its own edit control. A new `get_teams_with_unviewed_notes` RPC drives a Home nudge banner for unreviewed notes, and `GoalsSubnav`'s History pill gained a small red-dot indicator — the underlying `anyUnviewed` computation and "Mark all as viewed" bulk action already existed correctly and needed no changes, confirmed by reading the code first. `HistoryViewer` (the older, Month/Home-reachable component, left otherwise untouched) got one narrowly-scoped addition: a new `fetchPracticeActualStart` fetch so it shows a practice's real run time instead of its scheduled time when run substantially before or after the scheduled slot.

**One real, if unconfirmed-in-practice, correctness bug found and fixed while reading `saveSessionGroups`, and one extended false-alarm investigation that concluded no code fix was needed:** see the two new Gotchas entries above for full detail. In short — `saveSessionGroups` zipped its `.insert().select()` response against the request array by index rather than by the row's own `group_number`, a latent risk given Supabase/PostgREST doesn't guarantee response-row order matches request order; fixed to zip by key. Separately, a live symptom during verification (stale-looking station groups after a player move, apparent `saveSessionGroups` errors in the console, zero matching DB rows) triggered a real investigation — checked RLS policies and their dependencies, reproduced both single- and batch-row inserts directly against the REST API with the test account's real JWT (both succeeded, ruling out RLS/schema), and ultimately traced the DB symptom to the investigation's *own* manual `curl`-inserted probe rows polluting the "latest by `created_at`" resolution the app's own `fetchLatestGroups` relies on. After cleaning those rows up and re-testing from a genuinely clean state (fresh seed, fresh page load, an in-page `console.error` patch to rule out stale buffered console output — the same known `read_console_messages` staleness gotcha, reconfirmed a third time), the write path was confirmed working correctly end-to-end via a direct DB check. No code change resulted from this beyond the already-mentioned zip-order fix.

**Verified live** across two disposable coach accounts (a smaller architecture-focused pass, then a more thorough second pass seeding a team with 3 players, a location, an upcoming practice with a station block for Practice Setup/live-session testing, and a separately-fabricated completed practice with real session/log/attendance/note rows for History testing): the smart Join Practice button correctly relabeling and routing into an existing session rather than starting a new one; the full Practice Setup screen (attendance, station leader assignment, live interactive player reassignment, confirmed via a direct DB query showing the move landed with the correct `group_id`/`player_id`); Start Practice correctly transitioning every viewer to the live view with the new header layout; the "Up Next" clickable preview correctly reflecting live-reassigned groups rather than stale planned ones; the block-intro dark theme and single-button Reshuffle flow; only one `practice_live_sessions` row ever existing despite multiple repeated "Join Practice" visits (independently re-confirming the atomic RPC's race-safety under real, if manual, conditions); the rebuilt History detail screen end to end (Run Now, Save Template, single edit-mode toggle correctly gating every editable control, notes grouped under their drill); the Home unread-notes nudge banner; and the History tab's red dot. Both disposable accounts and every seeded row were fully torn down afterward (SQL cascade cleanup + `admin.deleteUser` for each), and the `rop-dev` preview server was stopped.

### Thirty-second session (2026-08-04) — 8-item Builder/Home direct-feedback batch: equipment tracking, out-player enforcement, station-block friction, duration pill toggle, Practice Components defaults, Run Now gate, cancel dialog wording

Jax gave a single, dense 7-item feedback message (split into 8 tracked items) after using the app live: (1) copying a drill (or adding one straight from Explore) when missing a piece of equipment and choosing "Add Anyway" silently deleted the equipment reference instead of keeping it visible somewhere the coach could act on later, and Builder's own add-to-practice flow needed the same prompt-and-flag treatment, surfaced on Practice Setup and the public Practice Preview link too; (2) Home's Upcoming Practices should show the next 4 practices regardless of how far out they are, and the separate "N practices in the next 14 days need a plan" button should go away; (3) a player marked out for a practice still showed as pickable inside Builder's station editor; (4) selecting a Group By option or a library drill inside a station block kicked the coach out of the block entirely, and clicking Done after editing a long block scrolled to a random spot in the library instead of back to the Run of Practice; (5) the planned/target duration pill in Builder should be tappable to switch between "X/Y min planned" and "N more minutes needed"; (6) Practice Components should default to Intro/Closer/Station Block for a new coach, with the blank grid quadrant (on an odd count) showing helpful light-green text instead of empty space; (7) Run Now in Builder shouldn't be available more than an hour before a scheduled practice; (8) the cancel-practice confirm dialog had an em dash and unclear button labels.

**Built**, roughly in the order above: (2) `HomeScreen.jsx`'s `upcomingCandidates` replaced the old 14-day-bounded `windowCandidates`, sorted ascending and sliced to the next 4 unrun practices regardless of date; the "needs a plan" nudge block and its `needsPlanning` computation were removed outright. (3) `StationConfig` (`ActivityConfigs.jsx`) takes a new `absentPlayerIds` prop (already-fetched `absentPlayerIds` state in `BuilderScreen`, previously only used for new-activity defaults, now threaded further in), disables/grays/labels each absent player's chip across every station, auto-strips them from any station's `assignments` via a `useEffect` keyed on the absent-id set (covers a player marked out *after* the block already had assignments), and excludes them from `genRandom`/`groupByPosition`/`groupByHand`. (4) The outside-click "collapse this row" listener switched from `.closest()`/`.contains()` to `event.composedPath()` (see new Gotcha); `collapseAndScroll` nests a second `requestAnimationFrame` before its `scrollIntoView` call. (5) A new `pillShowRemaining` toggle state drives the existing `.pill`/`.pill.over`/`.pill.exceeds` duration display, rendered as a real `<button>` (was a static `<span>`) only when the practice has a target duration to toggle against. (6) `getVisibleComponentTypes()` (`constants.js`) falls back to the 3 `defaultOn:true` types instead of all 7; a conditional `.li.tap` filler cell (light-green, dashed border, opens the components picker on tap) fills the blank quadrant whenever the visible-type count is odd. (7) `runTooFarAway` (a simple `scheduledMoment` diff against now, > 60 minutes) gates Builder's Run Now button with `disabled`+explanatory `title` — Practice Detail's own Run Now button deliberately untouched, since the request was specifically about Builder. (8) `PracticeDetail.jsx`'s cancel confirm box lost its em dash and got real verb-phrase buttons ("Never Mind" / "Cancel Practice" or "Cancel This Practice" / "Cancel This & Future" for a series).

(1) was the largest piece, spanning both a new column and three separate surfaces: `assets.acquired boolean not null default true` (`20260805020000_assets_acquired_flag.sql`) lets an asset be "known about but not yet owned." `resolveEquipmentAgainstPool` (`supabase.js`, already shared by `copyDrillToMyLibrary` and Builder's `resolveDrillEquipmentForCoach`) was extended to branch on whether an equipment id already belongs to the caller's own pool — update the existing asset's `acquired` flag in place if so (never a duplicate row), otherwise create a new asset in the caller's pool with `acquired` set per the coach's Add Equipment/Add Anyway choice, but *always* create something rather than silently dropping the reference the way the old code did. `findMissingEquipment` now also treats `acquired:false` as a match failure, so the existing `EquipmentMismatchDialog` (both `context="library"` and `context="practice"`) correctly re-prompts on a later add until the coach resolves it, with no dialog-level changes needed. Surfaced: the Edit Drill modal's Team Equipment/Player Gear chip pickers (`ModalLayer.jsx`) now show an amber "· Need to acquire" chip plus an inline "✓ Got it" action for any selected-but-unacquired item; Practice Detail's equipment summary card and per-activity/per-station equipment lines (`PracticeDetail.jsx`) flag unacquired items in bold red with "NOT ACQUIRED"/"(not acquired)"; and the public Practice Preview link required a real RPC change — `get_preview_view` (`20260805030000_preview_view_equipment_acquired.sql`) now returns each equipment item as `{name,acquired}` instead of a bare name string, with `CommandScreen.jsx`'s `PreviewView` rendering unacquired items as a red-tinted chip with "· not acquired" in the Equipment Needed summary and inline per-activity/per-station.

**Verified live**, one disposable coach account seeded directly via SQL (a team, 3 players, one asset already owned and one seeded `acquired:false` to simulate a prior "Add Anyway," a drill referencing both, a second equipment-free drill, and 5 upcoming practices spread from 20 minutes to 25 days out, one with a planned absence and a pre-built station block): confirmed Home's Upcoming Practices showed exactly the next 4 (today/tomorrow/Thu/three-weeks-out), correctly omitting the 5th/furthest one, with no "needs a plan" button anywhere; confirmed the unacquired asset showed flagged on the Practice Setup screen (reached via Home's own "Practice Setup" button, which routes to the /preview/:token screen) with "Agility Ladder · not acquired" in both the summary and inline; confirmed the out player showed "Out: Jordan R" on Practice Detail and, inside Builder's station editor, a disabled/grayed/strikethrough chip with the exact tooltip text, consistent across both stations in the block; confirmed selecting "Group By... Position" and then a different library drill for the station both kept the station block's own editor open throughout (no kick-out), and that clicking Done afterward scrolled back to "The Run of Practice" section, not into the library; confirmed the duration pill (after setting a `scheduled_duration_minutes` via SQL, since none of the seeded practices had one) toggled from "11/45 min" to "Needs 34 more min" and back on repeated taps; confirmed Practice Components showed exactly Intro/Closer/Station Block with the light-green hint filling the blank 4th quadrant; confirmed Run Now was disabled with the correct tooltip on a practice 3 hours out and (implicitly, by the disabled-button pattern being purely time-driven) would enable within the 1-hour window; confirmed the cancel dialog's new copy and button labels; and confirmed triggering Builder's own equipment-mismatch dialog by adding the equipment-bearing drill, choosing "Add Drill Anyway," and confirming both Cones and the still-unacquired Agility Ladder remained linked to the newly-added activity (green/selected) rather than the missing one being dropped. Disposable account and all seeded rows fully torn down afterward (SQL cascade + `admin.deleteUser`) — no known gaps hit during teardown this time.

### Thirty-first session (2026-08-03) — untagged-message swap, Delete Practice, no-date time editing, window-weeks relocated

Jax gave a 4-item feedback batch on Goals & Insights: (1) once a coach has gone and tagged every drill, the "link drills to the library" nudge should say something more useful instead of repeating advice already followed; (2) History needed a way to delete a practice from the top of the session detail screen; (3) editing a logged drill's actual time popped an unnecessary native date picker -- wanted minutes/start-time/end-time editing instead; (4) the "measure over the last N weeks" control sits in the Goals (target-setting) card but actually belongs with the report below it.

**Built:** (1) `GoalsScreen.jsx` computes `allDrillsTagged` (same sport/owner-scoped untagged-drill count the Library deep link itself already used) and `GlanceView` swaps in an accurate message with the CTA hidden once there's nothing left in the library to tag. (2) A "Delete Practice" button at the top of `SessionHistoryDetail` (`canManage` only, real confirm first) calls the same `archivePractice()` every other "Delete" in the app uses. (3) `TimeRangeForm` replaced its two `type="datetime-local"` inputs with time-only Start/End plus a Duration (min) field, all three kept consistent (editing Start shifts End, preserving duration; editing End or Duration recomputes the other, holding Start fixed) -- the date itself is derived invisibly from the log row's own date or the session's end date, never picked by the coach. (4) The window-weeks control moved from `GoalsEditor` into `GlanceView`'s own header, gated on `canManage`.

**Two real bugs found and fixed during the build/verification, not just the four requested changes:**
1. **`get_team_session_history` never excluded archived practices.** Its `sessions` CTE joined on `team_id`/`status` only, with no `archived_at` check, unlike every other practice-listing query in the app — so the new "Delete Practice" button would have archived the practice but the session kept showing up in History regardless. Fixed in `20260805010000_session_history_excludes_deleted_practice.sql` (dry-run verified, checked byte-identical against live via `pg_get_functiondef` before editing, matching this file's own documented drift-checking convention).
2. **`updateGoalsWindowWeeks` never refreshed the top-level `data.teams` cache.** Moving the window control surfaced this immediately: after saving, the on-screen value looked right until navigating away and back (a fresh mount re-derives from the stale cached `team.goalsWindowWeeks`), at which point it silently reverted even though the database already had the new value the whole time — confirmed by querying the database directly while the UI showed the old number. Fixed by threading `refreshTeams` down (`TeamGoalsRoute` → `GoalsScreen` → `GlanceView`) and calling it alongside the existing `refreshReport()` after every save. See the new Gotcha above for the general pattern.

**Verified live**, one disposable coach account with a fully fabricated completed practice (real `practices`/`practice_activities`/`practice_live_sessions`/`session_activity_log`/`session_attendance` rows, one tagged drill and one untagged drill, seeded directly via SQL rather than driving a full live-run through the UI): confirmed the untagged-time warning showed with the CTA before tagging, then swapped to the "all tagged" reassurance message with the CTA hidden after tagging the one remaining drill (and confirmed the minutes correctly re-attributed from Untagged to Shooting immediately, since attribution is computed live from current tags, not a snapshot); confirmed the window-weeks control saves, survives both a client-side remount and a hard reload, and updates the report's own percentages; confirmed the time-editing form shows no date picker, that editing Duration correctly recomputes End while Start stays fixed, and that a save updates the scorecard and marks the row "adjusted"; confirmed Delete Practice's confirm dialog fires with the right message, blocks on cancel, and on confirm removes the session from History immediately. Test data teardown hit the already-documented `session_attendance.player_id` FK gap again — this time discovering neither `authenticated` nor `service_role` has any `DELETE` grant on that table at all, worked around via the `supabase db query --linked` Management-API path instead of a service-role client (see the Known Gaps entry).

### Thirtieth session (2026-08-03) — Skill Tag picker keyboard/lost-tag fix, Create Template margins + inline location creation

Jax gave a 2-item feedback batch: (1) in the Add/Edit Drill modal, typing a new skill tag name had two real problems — the on-screen keyboard could cover the input with no way to scroll back to it, and tapping the sheet's "Done" button before "Add" silently discarded whatever had been typed, with no warning; (2) Library → Templates → Create Template had margins far too tight (a recurring class of issue this file has fixed several times elsewhere) and its Default Location field had no way to add a location inline, unlike Builder's.

**Built:** (1) `SkillTagPicker` (`ModalLayer.jsx`) merged its separate search field and bottom "add a new tag" row into one field — typing something with no exact existing match now shows a compact category-select + "+ Add" button immediately below the search box, always visible regardless of keyboard height, since there's no longer a second input buried under a long scrollable chip list. Both the sheet's "Done" buttons now route through a `tryClose()` check that calls `window.confirm` (same lightweight pattern already used for unsaved-notes/unsaved-goals/unsaved-template warnings elsewhere in this app) before discarding unsubmitted, addable text. (2) `TemplateWorkspace`'s wrapping div gained the `padding:"0 16px"` every other Library sub-screen already has. Its Default Location field gained the exact same "+ Add New Location..." dropdown option / "+ Add a Location" zero-locations fallback button Builder's own Location field uses, opening the same `AddLocationDialog` and auto-selecting the new location.

**Verified live**, one disposable coach account: confirmed typing a brand-new skill tag name shows the inline category-select + Add button directly under the search box with no scrolling required; confirmed tapping Done with unsubmitted text triggers the exact expected confirm message and, when cancelled, leaves the sheet open with the text intact; confirmed tapping Add creates and selects the tag, clears the field, and a subsequent Done closes with no confirm prompt. For Create Template: confirmed the screen's margins now match sibling Library screens; confirmed the zero-locations "+ Add a Location" button opens `AddLocationDialog`, and that saving a location both auto-selects it as the Default Location and adds a "+ Add New Location..." option to the now-populated dropdown. Test account and its team fully deleted afterward; temporary setup/cleanup scripts removed from the repo.

### Twenty-ninth session (2026-08-03) — margins fix, untagged-drills tagging flow, Development Pulse collapse/persist/new-insight-dot

Jax gave a 3-item feedback batch after using Development Pulse and Goals & Insights live: (1) Overview/Trends/History's side margins read too tight compared to sibling team tabs, (2) the Overview tab's untagged-time section should have a CTA into a filtered, tag-as-you-go view of the team's untagged drills, with a way back, and (3) Development Pulse should be collapsible with its collapsed state persisted across sessions, plus a small red dot when a new insight arrives while collapsed.

**Built:** (1) `TeamGoalsRoute` was the one per-team route missing the `16px 16px` wrapper Roster/Equipment both use — added it, removed the now-redundant duplicate bottom padding `GoalsScreen.jsx` had on its own top-level div. (2) A "Tag Untagged Drills" button (Overview, `canManage` only) navigates to `/library` with `location.state.untaggedForSport`/`teamId`/`returnTo`; `NewLibraryScreen.jsx` reads that once on arrival, scopes "My Drills" to the team's sport and (by default) to zero-tag drills, and shows a "Back to Goals & Insights" button. No extra plumbing was needed to "return to the untagged list" after tagging a drill — the filter is a plain derived value recomputed from `data.activityLibrary`, so a freshly-tagged drill just falls out of the list on its own next render, in place. (3) `DevelopmentPulseCard.jsx` gained a caret that collapses it to just its header; collapsed/expanded state and a "last-seen state fingerprint" (`teamId|state|categoryId`) both persist in `localStorage` keyed by `coachId` (one physical widget, not per-team); a small red dot shows next to the header whenever collapsed and the fingerprint has changed since last seen, clearing automatically the moment the card is (or becomes) expanded.

**One real, pre-existing bug found and fixed during live verification, unrelated to any of the three requests**: the `insufficient_history` state's goal-mix bar (`GoalMixStrip`) read `c.targetPct` (camelCase) off raw `report.skills` rows, which actually carry `target_pct` (snake_case) — every segment's width computed to `NaN`, so the bar rendered as nothing at all, and its `aria-label` read literally "Passing undefined%, Shooting undefined%." This has been broken since the twenty-eighth session shipped; nothing in that session's own verification matrix happened to exercise this specific state's graphic closely enough to catch it. Fixed by mapping to camelCase at the call site (`{skillCategoryId, name, targetPct}`), matching the prop convention every other graphic in the file already uses.

**Verified live**, one disposable coach account + team (real Soccer skill categories, real seeded goals, two library drills — one tagged, one not): confirmed Overview/Trends/History margins now match Roster's; confirmed the untagged CTA lands on a correctly sport-and-tag-scoped Library view, confirmed tagging the one untagged drill via the existing Edit → Skill Tags flow made it disappear from the list automatically with no navigation, confirmed "Back to Goals & Insights" returns to the right team's Overview tab; confirmed the Development Pulse caret collapses/expands, confirmed collapsed state survives a hard reload, confirmed the red dot appears after mutating team goals directly in the database while the card was collapsed and clears on expand, confirmed the goal-mix bar renders correctly (bar and aria-label both) after the fix. All test data (team, drills, tags, the one auth user) confirmed fully deleted via a residue query afterward; temporary setup/cleanup scripts removed from the repo.

**Continued, same day — Permissions toggle stale-state bug.** Jax reported "Share Drill Library" and "Share Practice Planning" (`PermissionsModal.jsx`) didn't visually reflect a tap until the modal was closed and reopened. Root cause (see the new Gotcha above for the general pattern): `App.jsx`'s `permissionsCoach` state held the *whole coach object*, snapshotted once when the roster row was clicked — toggling correctly called the right RPC and `refreshTeams()`, which updated `data.teams` with a fresh coach object, but nothing ever reassigned `permissionsCoach` itself, so the already-open modal kept reading the stale pre-toggle value. Fixed by renaming to `permissionsCoachId` (just the id) and deriving the live coach object from `team.coaches` on every render, so the modal reflects the real value the instant `refreshTeams()` resolves. Verified live with a disposable head coach + assistant pair (a real invite → accept relationship, not synthetic staff rows): opened Permissions on the assistant, toggled both "Share Drill Library" and "Share Practice Planning" on, confirmed both switches visibly flipped without closing the modal, then toggled both back off and confirmed the same in reverse. Both test accounts and their team fully deleted afterward.

### Twenty-eighth session (2026-08-03) — Development Pulse, a dynamic Home CTA for Goals & Insights

Jax provided a spec doc (`RUN-OF-PRACTICE-DEVELOPMENT-PULSE-HOME-WIDGET.md`) for a new Home widget: a single, dynamic card resolving to exactly one of 7 states, shown in Coach mode beneath the next-practice hero, with a bullet-bar graphic and one CTA that deep-links to the exact useful place — including opening Builder with Goal Guidance pre-expanded and a specific category highlighted. The spec's own explicit constraints: reuse the twenty-sixth session's shared goal-attribution math exactly (no "fifth variation" of goal math), avoid N+1 queries, no new charting dependency, named threshold constants, live verification before calling it done.

**Built:** one extended RPC (`get_team_goal_report` gained `usable_actual_session_count`, verified byte-identical to live via `pg_get_functiondef` before editing — not trusted to migration-history bookkeeping alone, per this file's own documented drift history) plus one new batch RPC (`get_teams_recent_completed_session(p_team_ids[])`, one call across every candidate team rather than per-team, used only when there's no next practice to short-circuit focus-team selection). Two pure helpers in `constants.js` (`resolveDevelopmentPulseFocusTeamId`, `resolveDevelopmentPulseState`) — presentation-neutral, deterministic, independently unit-tested (19 cases covering all 7 states, the aligned-but-moving-away override, tie-breaking, and focus-team priority — all passing). A separate `presentState()` in the new `DevelopmentPulseCard.jsx` translates a resolved state into copy/CTA, keeping the resolver itself free of JSX/routing concerns. Bullet-bar graphic, goal-mix strip, and completeness bar are all inline SVG — no Recharts, matching the Trends chart's existing convention. Wired into `HomeScreen.jsx` (Coach mode only, `!isOrgMode &&` gate — same pattern as every other mode-gated surface in this file), `BuilderScreen`/`BuilderGoalGuidance.jsx` (force-open + one-time category highlight on arrival), and `GoalsScreen.jsx` (deep-link to the Trends subtab, or a one-time pulse on the Untagged row when there's no precise in-page anchor).

**Two real bugs found and fixed during live verification, not just the planned build:**
1. **A spec-violating over-duration recommendation.** The "missing from next plan" state's `Plan {N} minutes` CTA didn't check `closable` the way the sibling "meaningful gap" state already did — live-verified as an actual "Plan 63 minutes" CTA on a 45-minute practice, which the spec explicitly prohibits ("Do not use `Plan N minutes` with a number larger than the practice duration"). Fixed to fall back to the same "This gap will take more than one practice to close" / "Review Goal Guidance" copy the closable-gap branch already used.
2. **A navigation bug that silently loaded the wrong practice.** The Builder CTA's first version called `navigate()` directly instead of the existing `goToBuilder()` helper, which left `BuilderScreen`'s `useState` initializers reading a stale `null` `editPracticeId` on first render (that context value is only set a tick later, by `BuilderRoute`'s own effect, specifically because every *other* existing entry point already calls `goToBuilder()` first) — Builder opened for the right practice ID in the URL but rendered blank-practice defaults (today's date, no duration, empty plan) instead of the real saved one. See the new Gotcha above for the full mechanism. Fixed by extending `goToBuilder()` with an optional `navState` param instead of adding a second, parallel way into Builder.

**A third, unrelated bug found while tearing down test data**: `session_activity_log.practice_activity_id`/`.station_id` had no `ON DELETE` action at all, unlike every other link in that cascade chain — blocking deletion of a team/practice's history the moment any activity had been logged against it. Confirmed via dry-run (`BEGIN;...ROLLBACK;`), then applied to production after explicit sign-off (`20260805000000_session_activity_log_activity_cascade.sql`) since this session's sandbox blocks direct non-dry-run schema writes without it.

**Verified live** with one disposable coach account + team (real Soccer skill categories/tags, not synthetic ones) walked through several states via direct data mutation rather than 7 separate synthetic teams: `missing_from_next_plan` (confirmed the closable-CTA fix, confirmed Builder opens with Goal Guidance expanded and the right category pulsed), `aligned` (confirmed "View Trends" lands on the Trends subtab), `data_quality` (confirmed "Review Untagged Time" lands on Goals Overview with the Untagged row pulsed), and the aligned-but-moving-away override (found unintentionally — a next-practice plan skewed entirely toward one category pushed that category "Farther from goal," correctly overriding a would-be `aligned` state, exactly as the spec's own override rule intends). Phone-width layout (375px) confirmed clean. Org-mode suppression confirmed by code inspection only (the gate is a trivial, consistent `!isOrgMode &&` boolean already used everywhere else in this codebase) — not re-tested against a live org account, since this disposable account had no organization. All test data (team, drills, 3 stray auth users left over from earlier failed setup attempts) confirmed fully deleted via a final residue query, not just assumed.

### Twenty-seventh session (2026-08-02) — 14-item direct-feedback batch from testing the twenty-sixth session's work live

Jax tested the Goals & Insights enhancements and the Permissions/Drill-Insights work live and came back with 14 items in one message, spanning a real permissions-default bug, a real CSS stacking-context bug, one new notification feature, one new Builder feature, one new drill-recommendation feature, one new sort feature, and several smaller copy/UX/color fixes. Organized into a task list and worked through all 14 in one pass, live-verifying the highest-risk ones (permissions defaults, the menu-clipping fix, the Builder switcher, the invite-accept notification, the drill-recommendation drill-down, and the goal-based sort) against real production data with two disposable test accounts (a head coach + an assistant, to exercise the real invite/accept/notification chain end to end), then tore both down.

**Two real bugs, not just requested changes:**
1. **Library sharing defaulted to shared, not private.** `head_coach_shares_library`/`assistant_shares_library` (added twenty-fifth session) both defaulted `true` — a head coach could see an assistant's whole library the moment an invite was accepted, with no opt-in step, contradicting the actual intent (browse via Explore, copy in what's wanted). Fixed the default to `false` (`20260803000000_library_sharing_default_off.sql`) and backfilled the 8 existing rows uniformly, confirmed via direct query that none had been explicitly toggled by anyone yet so backfilling didn't clobber a real choice.
2. **A drill's ellipsis dropdown was visually clipped by the next row.** Root-caused live (not guessed): `SortableActivityRow`'s per-row `position:relative` + explicit `zIndex` (present on every row, not just dragging/sticky ones, all at the same level) creates a new stacking context per row, which traps a `z-index:50` popover inside it — it can never escape to paint above a sibling row painted after it in DOM order, regardless of the popover's own z-index. See the new Gotcha above for the full mechanism. Fixed with a `raised` prop that lifts one specific row's zIndex above its siblings while its popover is open.

**The other 12, briefly (full detail in the Feature Inventory sections above):** Builder's drill picker now defaults to "My Library" with a switcher for the team's org/peer libraries instead of a flat unlabeled merge of everything accessible; a new `team_join_notices` table + Home card notifies the head coach when an invite is accepted and deep-links straight into `PermissionsModal` for that exact coach; the drill-menu private toggle is now "Keep Private"/"Make Public" (not "Hide from"/"Share with My Coaches" -- "Make Private" was already taken in the same menu) with a real confirm prompt before going public; a lock icon marks a private drill on its card; the heat-color card icon is gone (insights only via the ellipsis now, per "too distracting" feedback); Next Practice Guidance's below-target category rows expand to show which of the coach's own drills are tagged to that category; the Goals editor's Save button now grays out again immediately after a successful save; My Drills gained a Sort picker (Custom/Most Frequently Used/Alphabetical/Suggested-by-goals, the last ranking a drill by the largest deficit among its tagged categories for a team the coach picks); a small spacing fix between the new Goals subnav and the team tab row above it; a new amber "exceeds" planning-depth state (Home/Schedule pills and Builder's own pill) when planned drills add up to more than the scheduled duration, alongside the existing red/green under-planned states; and the global `/schedule` route now correctly underlines the Home tab (previously lit up none of them), with all active-tab underlines 50% thicker for PWA visibility.

**Verified live**, two disposable accounts (head coach + assistant) plus real seeded team/goals/drills (direct SQL, not click-through -- same reasoning as the twenty-sixth session's own verification: the point was exercising the new report/UI code against real data shapes, not re-testing already-shipped click paths): confirmed the Suggested sort correctly ranked a goal-tagged, high-deficit drill first; confirmed the ellipsis menu no longer clips and the wording/lock-icon/confirm-prompt all render correctly; confirmed Builder defaults to "My Library" with no switcher when there's only one source; confirmed the full invite → accept → notification → deep-linked Permissions modal chain end to end, including both toggles correctly defaulting off for the new relationship; confirmed a below-target category expands to the right drill; confirmed the Save button visibly grays out once nothing is dirty. Both accounts and all their data (including two drills, which needed the twenty-sixth session's own FK fix to hard-delete cleanly this time with zero manual SQL workaround needed) deleted afterward; temporary mint/seed/cleanup scripts removed from the repo.

### Twenty-sixth session (2026-08-02) — all five Goals & Insights enhancements from Jax's spec doc, in one pass

Jax handed over a full, detailed implementation spec (`RUN-OF-PRACTICE-GOALS-INSIGHTS-ENHANCEMENTS.md`) covering five enhancements: Skill-Development Trends, Next Practice Guidance, combined Builder Goal Guidance, the Practice Execution Scorecard, and Drill Performance Insights, plus explicit instructions to read this whole file first, inspect the live `get_team_goal_report` definition before writing any new math, dry-run every migration, and live-verify everything with disposable accounts. Asked two scoping questions up front — push through all five phases in one session vs. checkpoint after each, and what to do with an already-reorganized-but-uncommitted `BUILD-STATUS.md` sitting in the working tree — and got "push through all five" and "commit the reorg separately first," respectively; the reorg commit landed before any feature work started.

**Investigation before writing any SQL, per the spec's own instructions.** Read `get_team_goal_report`/`set_team_goals` (`20260730040000_fix_get_team_goal_report_stale_column.sql`, the most recent correct body) line by line rather than trusting the migration history alone, given this exact file's own documented history of migration-history-vs-live-schema drift. Confirmed via `npx supabase migration list` that local and remote migration bookkeeping matched exactly (no drift), and — since this sandbox has no Docker for `db diff`'s shadow database — used `supabase db query --linked` wrapped in `BEGIN;...ROLLBACK;` as the real dry-run mechanism instead (see new Gotcha above), including exercising real RLS/authorization by setting `request.jwt.claim.sub` to a real user's uuid inside the rolled-back transaction. Traced practice-activity type constraints (`'activity'/'station_block'/'checklist'/'break'`), confirmed the shipped "Practice Components" system (Water Break, Stretch, etc.) all use `type:"checklist"` client-side despite a `'break'` type existing in the schema for exactly this purpose — a pre-existing, unrelated-to-this-session quirk, not something to fix here, but load-bearing knowledge for getting the new attribution math to match existing behavior exactly rather than inventing a new split (the spec's own explicit instruction).

**Phase 1 — shared math layer** (`20260802000000_goal_attribution_shared_helpers.sql`): extracted `get_team_goal_report`'s attribution rules into four `security definer`, `revoke all from public` SQL functions plus one composite type — see the new "Goals & Insights architecture" theme above for the full reasoning, including why the revoke matters (these have no authorization check of their own).
